https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum.lean#L1-L111
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.HostFrameEx

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr
open scoped Pointwise

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]
  [CharP (ResidueField O) l]

variable (Ω O) in

abbrev omO : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := GC.cycF ℚ Ω l (ResidueField O)

structure Gen (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  M_ne : M ≠ 0
  l_dvd : l ∣ M
  two_dvd : 2 ∣ M
  cc : IsCC σ
  over_l : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
  quad : ∃ (D : ℕ) (δ : Ω), 0 < D ∧ (∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → q ∣ D → q ∣ M) ∧ δ ^ 2 = -(D : Ω) ∧ ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (η s : ℤ) • δ
  cyc : ∃ a : ℕ, Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) η ∧ Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) (barChar O Ψ)
  η_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker
  even : barChar O Ψ σ = 1
  Ψ_l : ∃ j : ℕ, ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', barChar O Ψ s = omO Ω l O s ^ j
  g4 : ¬ (omO Ω l O).ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ≤ η.ker
  g5 : ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B, η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  Hb_open : IsOpen (Hb : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  Hb_ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hb
  Hb_idx : ∃ a : ℕ, Hb.index = l ^ a
  Hb_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hb
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ Hb, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1

variable (Ω) in

def Hth (w : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l} ∩ {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l}
  one_mem' := ⟨⟨1, by simp⟩, ⟨1, by simp⟩⟩
  mul_mem' := by
    rintro s t ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, ⟨a', ha'⟩⟩ ⟨⟨b, hb⟩, ⟨b', hb'⟩⟩
    refine ⟨⟨a * b, fun ζ h => ?_⟩, ⟨b' * a', fun ζ h => ?_⟩⟩
    · rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb ζ h, map_pow, ha ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
    · rw [mul_inv_rev, AlgEquiv.mul_apply, ha' ζ h, map_pow, hb' ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
  inv_mem' := by
    rintro s ⟨h1, h2⟩
    exact ⟨h2, by show ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l; rw [inv_inv]; exact h1⟩

variable (Ω) in

def Hlay (m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)).ker

structure FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (M : ℕ) (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (H₀ : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  idx : H₀.index = 2
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ H₀
  loc : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ H₀ = Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ η.ker
  ram : ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 ∧ ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ H₀

structure FrobeniusWitnessPrime (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M w : ℕ) : Prop where
  prime : w.Prime
  cop : ¬ w ∣ M
  one : w ≡ 1 [MOD l]
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 →
    Dec (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (omO Ω l O).ker

def Upl (Hb H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m

structure UFacts (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M m : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ U
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ U
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  mu : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s ≠ 1
  deg : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → l ^ m ≤ U.relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) B')
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s = barChar O Ψ s ^ j

abbrev etaO (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ :=
  (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (ResidueField O)).toMonoidHom).comp η

def StrictCl (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (κ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop :=
  ∃ y : G ℚ Ω → ResidueField O, IsLocallyConstant y ∧ (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) ∧
    (¬ ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ U, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    (∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B', y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, η s = 1 → barChar O Ψ s = 1 → y s = 0

def Linked (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ), ∃ S π ι pl e c, GC.FLink (F := W.Lp L) (Ω := Ω) l Φ S
      ((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) π ι k.pl pl e c ∧
      (∀ (g : G (W.Lp L) Ω) (h : galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ∈ upl Ω L), π g = k.p ⟨_, h⟩) ∧
      ∀ v s, galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω (ι v s) = k.j v s

def HasFrameW (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M Q : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ w : ℕ), Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w ∧ w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] ∧ upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w

end UpperTheory.V.Fr

end


[MFC_SRC_FRAME_DEFS] Actual Frame, cone cohomology, all 28 Ok fields and Big
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ContinuousCoboundaries2.lean#L1-L216
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.FramedTwoCoboundaries
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.UnitaryThreeReflectionRight

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

section Cone

variable {k G W V : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation k G W) (b : V → Submodule k W)

def coneH0 : Submodule k W where
  carrier := {X | (∀ g, ρ g X = X) ∧ ∀ v, X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_add, a1, b1], fun v => add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun g => map_zero _, fun v => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_smul, a1], fun v => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v)⟩

def Z1C : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ s t, x.1 (s * t) = x.1 s + ρ s (x.1 t)) ∧
    ∀ v s, x.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.2 v) - x.2 v) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun s t => ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun s t => by simp [a2], fun v s => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries1 : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ X : W, (∀ s, x.1 s = ρ s X - X) ∧ ∀ v, x.2 v - X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨X, a1, a2⟩ ⟨Y, b1, b2⟩
    refine ⟨X + Y, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a1, b1, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨X, a1, a2⟩
    refine ⟨c • X, fun s => by simp [a1, smul_sub], fun v => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

abbrev H1C : Type := Z1C loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b).comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype

def continuousCocycles2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (x.2 v)) ∧
    (∀ s t u, ρ s (x.1 (t, u)) + x.1 (s, t * u) = x.1 (s * t, u) + x.1 (s, t)) ∧
    ∀ v s t, x.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (x.2 v) (s, t) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3, b4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun v => (a2 v).add (b2 v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]
      rw [add_add_add_comm, a3, b3, add_add_add_comm]
    · convert add_mem (a4 v s t) (b4 v s t) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, dOne, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp [dOne]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun v => (a2 v).comp (c • ·), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, ← smul_add, a3]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a4 v s t) using 1
      simp [dOne, smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (β : G → W) (Y : V → W), IsLocallyConstant β ∧ x.1 = dOne ρ β ∧
    ∀ v s, x.2 v s - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨β', Y', b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨β + β', Y + Y', a1.add b1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, dOne, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by funext p; simp [dOne], by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨c • β, c • Y, a1.comp (c • ·), ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp [a2, dOne, smul_sub]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
      simp [smul_sub]

abbrev coneH2 : Type := continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ b).comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b).subtype

end Cone

def chRep {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) : Representation k G k where
  toFun g := (χ g : k) • LinearMap.id
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext; simp [mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem chRep_apply {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) (x : k) :
    chRep χ g x = χ g * x := rfl

structure Frame (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] : Type 1 where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]
  [top : TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [tg : IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
  [cpt : CompactSpace Γ]
  [t2 : T2Space Γ]
  [td : TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ]
  del : Γ →* ℤˣ
  c : Γ
  V : Type
  [fV : Fintype V]
  Gv : V → Type
  [gv : ∀ v, Group (Gv v)]
  [tv : ∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  [tgv : ∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)]
  [cv : ∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)]
  [t2v : ∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)]
  [tdv : ∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)]
  loc0 : ∀ v, Gv v →* del.ker
  Iv : ∀ v, Subgroup (Gv v)
  dv : V → ℕ
  Inf : Type
  [fI : Fintype Inf]
  cw : Inf → Γ
  eps : Γ →* ℤ_[l]ˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* kˣ
  g0 : del.ker

attribute [instance] Frame.grp Frame.top Frame.tg Frame.cpt Frame.t2 Frame.td Frame.fV Frame.gv Frame.tv Frame.tgv
  Frame.cv Frame.t2v Frame.tdv Frame.fI

namespace Frame

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k)

abbrev G0 : Type := F.del.ker

def loc (v : F.V) : F.Gv v →* F.Γ := F.del.ker.subtype.comp (F.loc0 v)

def conj : F.G0 →* F.G0 := (MulAut.conjNormal F.c : MulAut F.G0).toMonoidHom

def star (g : F.G0) : F.G0 := F.conj g⁻¹

def loc2 (p : F.V × Bool) : F.Gv p.1 →* F.G0 := if p.2 then F.conj.comp (F.loc0 p.1) else F.loc0 p.1

def d : ℕ := ∑ v, F.dv v

def om (K : Type) [CommRing K] [CharP K l] : F.Γ →* Kˣ :=
  (Units.map ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K).comp PadicInt.toZMod).toMonoidHom).comp F.eps

def delK (K : Type) [CommRing K] : F.Γ →* Kˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom K).toMonoidHom).comp F.del

def Nv (v : F.V) : Subgroup (F.Gv v) := F.Iv v ⊓ (F.eps.comp (F.loc v)).ker

def ext {k' : Type} [Field k'] (f : k →+* k') : Frame l k' :=
  { F with Ψ := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ }

def EC : Prop :=
  ∀ (K W : Type) [Field K] [Finite K] [CharP K l] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W]
    (ρ : Representation K F.Γ W) (b : F.V → Submodule K W), IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set F.Γ) →
    (∀ v s, ∀ X ∈ b v, ρ (F.loc v s) X ∈ b v) →
    (∀ f : W →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (ρ g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0) →
    Module.Finite K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) ∧
    Module.finrank K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) + ∑ v, F.dv v * Module.finrank K (b v) ≤
      Module.finrank K (H1C F.loc ρ b) + ∑ w, Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (ρ (F.cw w) - 1))

structure Ok [CharP k l] (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite k
  del_open : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)
  c_sq : F.c * F.c = 1
  del_c : F.del F.c = -1
  loc_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc0 v)
  eps_cont : Continuous F.eps
  Ψ_open : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)
  Ψ_c : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  baseSubgroup_spec : F.Ψ F.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1
  phi : PhiCond F.Γ l
  card_inf : Fintype.card F.Inf = F.d
  cw_sq : ∀ w, F.cw w * F.cw w = 1
  del_cw : ∀ w, F.del (F.cw w) = -1
  f2a : ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g ≠ 1
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j * F.delK k g ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ v, ∃ g, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f4 : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f4' : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g = F.Ψ g ^ j
  Ψ_N : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1

  gen : ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (F.dv v) → F.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (F.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧
    (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ F.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.Gv v →* A, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (F.Gv v)) →
      (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, χ g = 1

  cl : ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* A,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ p : F.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv p.1, χ (F.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1
  ec : F.EC
  b1p : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  b1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc2 (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹.comp F.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  h1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (1 : F.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀

def Big (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop := 3 * C₀ + 5 + 2 < F.d ∧ ∀ v, 5 < F.dv v

end Frame

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[MFC_SRC_LINK_DEFS] Actual GalLink, HasFrame, SeedClause and original exits
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/HostFrameEx.lean#L1-L83

open scoped Pointwise

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def upl (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∀ x ∈ maximalRealSubfield L, s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x}
  one_mem' _ _ := rfl
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb x hx := by rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb x hx, ha x hx]
  inv_mem' {a} ha x hx := a.symm_apply_eq.2 (ha x hx).symm

variable {Ω} {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]

structure GalLink (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)) where
  ab : IsAbelianGalois ℚ L
  gal : galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L,
    barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  p : upl Ω L →* Φ.Γ
  p_cont : Continuous p
  p_surj : Function.Surjective p
  p_ker : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure {s : upl Ω L | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧
    s.1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ∧ η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s = 1}).topologicalClosure
  del : ∀ s, Φ.del (p s) = η s
  eps : ∀ s, Φ.eps (p s) = cycZ ℚ Ω l s
  psi : ∀ s, Φ.Ψ (p s) = barChar O Ψ s
  c : ∃ h : σ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨σ, h⟩ = Φ.c
  g0 : ∃ h : g₁ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨g₁, h⟩ = Φ.g0
  pl : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω
  pl_conj : ∀ v, ∃ γ : G ℚ Ω, η γ = 1 ∧ pl v = γ • B
  pl_all : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃! v, ∃ u ∈ upl Ω L, B' = u • pl v
  j : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* upl Ω L
  j_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (j v)
  j_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (j v)
  j_range : ∀ v (s : upl Ω L), s ∈ (j v).range ↔ s.1 ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  j_loc : ∀ v g, Φ.loc v g = p (j v g)
  j_ine : ∀ v g, g ∈ Φ.Iv v ↔ (j v g).1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = (upl Ω L).relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v))
  mu : ∀ v, ∃ g, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (j v g) ≠ 1

def GalLink.p0 {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
    {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ)
    (s : G L Ω) : Φ.G0 :=
  have h : galEmb ℚ L Ω s ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker := k.gal ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ L Ω s
  ⟨k.p ⟨galEmb ℚ L Ω s, h.1⟩, (k.del _).trans h.2⟩

def SeedClause (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : Type) (_ : Field K) (_ : NumberField K) (_ : Algebra K Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η' θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    Sd.FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L σ N w χ' η' θ j ∧ χ' ≠ η' ∧ ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' ∧ (∀ s, (η' s).val = (((η s).val : ℤ) : Qbar l)) ∧
    (∀ s, ‖((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s).val - (Ψ s).val.1‖ < 1) ∧ ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K

def HasFrame (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField c.O)) (C₀ : ℕ), Nonempty (GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      SeedClause c η Ψ σ L

def HostFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l]
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N)

def GdFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs)

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

end

structure Hypotheses₂ : Prop extends X.HcPublished where
  uo : UnramifiedOrthogonality
  gw : GreenbergWiles

def eqHom {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M]
    (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (D J : Subgroup Γ) : Submodule k (J → M) where
  carrier := {f | IsLocallyConstant f ∧ (∀ a b, f (a * b) = f a + f b) ∧
    ∀ d ∈ D, ∀ j j' : J, j'.1 = d * j.1 * d⁻¹ → f j' = ρ d (f j)}
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const _, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨ha.1.add hb.1, fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.2.1, hb.2.1]; abel,
    fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h, hb.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨ha.1.comp (c • ·), fun x y => by simp [ha.2.1], fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩

variable (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]

def cycF (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] : G F Ω →* kˣ :=
  (Units.map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k).toMonoidHom).comp (cycMod F Ω l 1)

variable {F Ω}

def clRk (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Cardinal := Module.rank (ZMod l) (Kum.XDual 1 U (ZMod l) (ZMod l))

def tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Subgroup (G F Ω) :=
  (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ v ∈ Sᶜ, ⋃ A ∈ {A : ValuationSubring Ω | IsPlaceOver A v},
    ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Set (G F Ω)))).topologicalClosure

structure GLink (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    (π : G F Ω →* Γ) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  above : ContainsAbove S l
  normal : K.Normal
  isOpen : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))
  idx : ¬ l ∣ K.index
  cont : Continuous π
  onto : Function.Surjective π
  ker : π.ker = tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K

structure LLink {V : Type} {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] (ι : ∀ v, Gv v →* G F Ω)
    (A : V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) : Prop where
  isOver : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) (pl v)
  inj : Function.Injective pl
  mem : ∀ w, w ∈ Set.range pl ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  cont : ∀ v, Continuous (ι v)
  ι_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (ι v)
  range : ∀ v, (ι v).range = Dec (F := F) (A v)

structure FLink {k : Type} [Field k] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω))
    (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))
    (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω) : Prop where
  real : IsTotallyReal F
  g : GLink l S K π
  only : ∀ w ∈ S, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  loc : LLink l ι A pl
  loc_eq : ∀ v s, Φ.loc v s = π (ι v s)
  ine : ∀ v, Φ.Iv v = (Ine (F := F) (A v)).comap (ι v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = locDeg (pl v)
  eps : ∀ g, Φ.eps (π g) = cycZ F Ω l g
  cc : ∀ w, IsCCAt (e w) (c w)
  cw : ∀ w, Φ.cw w = π (c w)
  del_open : IsOpen (Φ.del.ker : Set Φ.Γ)
  del_c : Φ.del Φ.c = -1
  K_del : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.del (π g) = 1
  K_Ψ : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.Ψ (π g) = 1

theorem gLink_quot (hl : l ≠ 2) {S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))} (hS : ContainsAbove S l) {K : Subgroup (G F Ω)}
    (h1 : K.Normal) (h2 : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))) (h3 : ¬ l ∣ K.index)
    [(tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K).Normal] :
    GLink l S K (QuotientGroup.mk' (tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K)) := by
  refine ⟨hl, hS, h1, h2, h3, ?_, QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective _, QuotientGroup.ker_mk' _⟩
  exact continuous_quot_mk

end UpperTheory.V.GC

section Frame

variable {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M] [TopologicalSpace M]
  [DiscreteTopology M] (F : Alg.Frame Γ) (ρ : Representation k Γ M)

def selF : Submodule k (H1 ρ) :=
  (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (resH1 ρ (F.φ v) (F.hφ v))) ⊓
    ⨅ q, (H1ur (ρ.comp (F.ψ q)) (F.N q)).comap (resH1 ρ (F.ψ q) (F.hψ q))

def shaF : Submodule k (H2 ρ) :=
  (⨅ v, LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ (F.φ v) (F.hφ v))) ⊓ ⨅ q, LinearMap.ker (resH2 ρ (F.ψ q) (F.hψ q))

  lt : LocalTateDuality

  gf : GlobalFiniteness
  ge : GlobalEulerChar
  pt : GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTateNormalised

section Gal

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def cycK (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ :=
  (Units.map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k).toMonoidHom).comp (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1)

variable {Ω}

def galFrame (SP : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (T Q : Finset ℕ) (v0 : T) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω)
    (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Alg.Frame (GS Ω SP) where
  T := T
  v0 := v0
  Gv v := Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  φ v := locHom SP (pl v)
  hφ v := continuous_locHom SP (pl v)
  Q := Q
  Gq q := Dec (F := ℚ) (pl q)
  ψ q := locHom SP (pl q)
  hψ q := continuous_locHom SP (pl q)
  N q := (Ine (F := ℚ) (pl q) ⊓ K).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl q))

structure GalData (l : ℕ) (SP : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (T Q : Finset ℕ) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω)
    (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  prime : ∀ p, p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q → p.Prime
  disj : Disjoint T Q
  l_mem : l ∈ T
  mem : ∀ v, v ∈ SP ↔ ∃ p, (p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q) ∧ ((p : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal
  isOver : ∀ p, p ∈ T ∨ p ∈ Q → (pl p).valuation (p : Ω) < 1
  idx : ∀ q ∈ Q, ¬ l ∣ K.relIndex (Ine (F := ℚ) (pl q))

def Van {k : Type} [Field k] (T : Finset ℕ) (pl : ℕ → ValuationSubring Ω) (K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (γ : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ) : Prop :=
  ∀ z : G ℚ Ω → k, IsLocallyConstant z → (∀ g h, z (g * h) = z g + (γ g : k) * z h) →
    (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, (∀ p ∈ T, ¬ A.valuation (p : Ω) < 1) → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, g ∈ K → z g = 0) →
    (∀ v ∈ T, ∃ m, ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v), z g = (γ g : k) * m - m) → ∃ m, ∀ g, z g = (γ g : k) * m - m

example (l : ℕ) (hl : l.Prime) (h2 : l ≠ 2) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) :
    GalData l {v | ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal} {l} ∅ (fun _ => A) ⊤ :=
  ⟨h2, by simpa using hl, by simp, by simp, by simp, by simpa using hA, by simp⟩

end Gal

[MFC_SRC_H0] exists_twistCharacter_continuousCohomology; same output via elementary CRT
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/EtaleSimplicialCochainSchemeSectionExtensionNatu.lean#L1-L140

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PlacesOfRationals.PlacesQ

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem galois_sqrt_sign {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (s : G ℚ Ω) {x : Ω} {n : ℤ} (hx : x ^ 2 = n) :
    s x = x ∨ s x = -x := by
  apply sq_eq_sq_iff_eq_or_eq_neg.1
  rw [← map_pow, hx, map_intCast]

private theorem decomposition_fixes_sqrt {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (B : ValuationSubring Ω) {x : Ω} {k : ℤ}
    (hx : x ^ 2 = ((1 + 4 * k : ℤ) : Ω)) (hk : B.valuation (k : Ω) < 1) {s : G ℚ Ω} (hs : s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B) : s x = x := by
  refine (galois_sqrt_sign s hx).resolve_right fun h => ?_
  set y : Ω := (1 + x) / 2 with hy
  have h1 : s y = 1 - y := by rw [hy, map_div₀, map_add, map_one, h, map_ofNat]; ring
  have h2 : y * (1 - y) = -k := by
    push_cast at hx
    rw [hy]; linear_combination (-1 / 4 : Ω) * hx
  have h3 : B.valuation (1 - y) < 1 ↔ B.valuation y < 1 := by rw [← h1]; exact valuation_apply_lt_one_iff hs y
  have h4 : B.valuation y < 1 := by
    by_contra h5
    have h6 : 1 ≤ B.valuation (y * (1 - y)) := by
      rw [map_mul]; exact one_le_mul (not_lt.1 h5) (not_lt.1 (h3.not.2 h5))
    rw [h2, Valuation.map_neg] at h6
    exact absurd hk (not_lt.2 h6)
  have h7 := B.valuation.map_add_lt h4 (h3.2 h4)
  simp at h7

private theorem etaleSimplicialCochain_scheme_section_extension_naturality {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (B : ValuationSubring Ω) {p n : ℕ} (hp : B.valuation (p : Ω) < 1)
    (h : p.Coprime n) : ¬ B.valuation (n : Ω) < 1 := by
  intro hn
  obtain ⟨a, b, hab⟩ := Nat.isCoprime_iff_coprime.2 h
  have e : (a : Ω) * p + b * n = 1 := by exact_mod_cast congrArg (Int.cast : ℤ → Ω) hab
  have hm : ∀ (c : ℤ) (z : Ω), B.valuation z < 1 → B.valuation (c * z) < 1 := fun c z hz => by
    rw [map_mul]
    exact (mul_le_of_le_one_left' ((B.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (intCast_mem B c))).trans_lt hz
  have := B.valuation.map_add_lt (hm a _ hp) (hm b _ hn)
  rw [e, map_one] at this
  exact lt_irrefl _ this

private theorem inertia_fixes_sqrt_discriminant {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (B : ValuationSubring Ω) {δ : Ω} {D : ℕ}
    (hδ : δ ^ 2 = -(D : Ω)) (hD : ¬ B.valuation ((4 * D : ℕ) : Ω) < 1) {s : G ℚ Ω} (hs : s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B) : s δ = δ := by
  refine (galois_sqrt_sign s (n := -D) (by rw [hδ]; simp)).resolve_right fun h => hD ?_
  have h1 : B.valuation δ ≤ 1 := by
    have : B.valuation δ ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by
      rw [← map_pow, hδ, Valuation.map_neg]; exact (B.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (natCast_mem B D)
    exact (pow_le_one_iff_of_nonneg zero_le' two_ne_zero).1 this
  have h2 := hs.2 δ h1
  have e : ((4 * D : ℕ) : Ω) = -((s δ - δ) ^ 2) := by rw [h]; push_cast; linear_combination (4 : Ω) * hδ
  rw [e, Valuation.map_neg, map_pow]
  exact pow_lt_one₀ zero_le' h2 two_ne_zero

private theorem gauss_sqrt_inertia_negates {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact p.Prime] (h4 : p % 4 = 3)
    (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (hB : B.valuation (p : Ω) < 1) :
    ∃ x : Ω, x ^ 2 = -(p : Ω) ∧ ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B, s x = -x := by
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have : NeZero (p : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hp.out.ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω p
  have h2 : ringChar (ZMod p) ≠ 2 := by rw [ZMod.ringChar_zmod_n]; omega
  let ψ := AddChar.zmodChar p hζ.pow_eq_one
  have hψ : ψ.IsPrimitive := AddChar.zmodChar_primitive_of_primitive_root p hζ
  let χ := (quadraticChar (ZMod p)).ringHomComp (Int.castRingHom Ω)
  have hχ : χ ≠ 1 := (MulChar.ringHomComp_ne_one_iff (RingHom.injective_int _)).2 (quadraticChar_ne_one h2)
  have hq : χ.IsQuadratic := (quadraticChar_isQuadratic _).comp _
  have hm : χ (-1) = -1 := by
    show ((quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-1) : ℤ) : Ω) = -1
    rw [quadraticChar_neg_one h2, ZMod.card, ZMod.χ₄_nat_three_mod_four h4]; simp
  have hmem : (-1 : (ZMod p)ˣ) ∈ (Ine (F := ℚ) B).map (B1.cycN ℚ Ω p) := by
    rw [B1.cycIne Ω p p B hB, B3.pPart, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    have : ordCompl[p] p = 1 := by rw [hp.out.factorization_self, pow_one, Nat.div_self hp.out.pos]
    have : Subsingleton (ZMod (ordCompl[p] p)) := by rw [this]; infer_instance
    exact Subsingleton.elim _ _
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hs'⟩ := hmem
  refine ⟨gaussSum χ ψ, by rw [gaussSum_sq hχ hq hψ, hm, ZMod.card]; simp, s, hs, ?_⟩
  have h3 := gaussSum_mulShift χ ψ (-1)
  rw [Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, hm, neg_one_mul, neg_eq_iff_eq_neg] at h3
  rw [← h3, gaussSum, gaussSum, map_sum]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
  rw [map_mul]
  congr 1
  · exact map_intCast s _
  · have h5 : ψ a ^ p = 1 := by rw [← AddChar.map_nsmul_eq_pow, nsmul_eq_mul, ZMod.natCast_self, zero_mul, AddChar.map_zero_eq_one]
    rw [B1.cycN_spec ℚ Ω p s h5, hs', ← AddChar.map_nsmul_eq_pow, nsmul_eq_mul, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val, AddChar.mulShift_apply]
    simp

private theorem complex_conjugation_fixes_sqrt {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {τ : G ℚ Ω} (h : IsCC τ) {z : Ω} {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
    (hz : z ^ 2 = n) : τ z = z := by
  refine (galois_sqrt_sign τ (n := n) (by rw [hz]; simp)).resolve_right fun h1 => ?_
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := h
  have h2 : starRingEnd ℂ (j z) = -j z := by rw [← map_neg, ← h1]; exact (hj z).symm
  have h3 := Complex.mul_conj (j z)
  rw [h2, mul_neg, ← sq, ← map_pow, hz, map_natCast] at h3
  have h4 : -(n : ℝ) = Complex.normSq (j z) := by exact_mod_cast h3
  have h5 := Complex.normSq_nonneg (j z)
  have h6 : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
  linarith

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.exists_twistCharacter_continuousCohomology :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∃ H₀, FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum η M H₀ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hη⟩ := hG.quad
  have hM := hG.M_ne
  obtain ⟨p, hlt, hp, hmod⟩ := Nat.forall_exists_prime_gt_and_zmodEq (4 * D + M) (q := 4 * M) (a := -1) (by omega)
    isCoprime_one_left.neg_left
  have : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hmod.dvd
  have ht' : -(p : ℤ) = 1 + 4 * (M * t) := by push_cast at ht; linear_combination ht
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := (B3.placesQ Ω).1 p hp
  obtain ⟨x, hx, s, hs, hsx⟩ := gauss_sqrt_inertia_negates p (by omega) A hA
  have hsδ : s δ = δ := inertia_fixes_sqrt_discriminant A hδ (etaleSimplicialCochain_scheme_section_extension_naturality A hA (Nat.coprime_of_lt_prime (by omega) (by omega) hp)) hs
  have hz : (δ * x) ^ 2 = ((D * p : ℕ) : Ω) := by rw [mul_pow, hδ, hx]; push_cast; ring
  have hpos : 0 < D * p := Nat.mul_pos hD hp.pos
  have hz0 : δ * x ≠ 0 := fun h => by
    rw [h, zero_pow two_ne_zero] at hz
    exact hpos.ne (by exact_mod_cast hz)
  have hne : -(δ * x) ≠ δ * x := fun h => hz0 (by linear_combination (-1 / 2 : Ω) * h)
  have hsz : s (δ * x) = -(δ * x) := by rw [map_mul, hsδ, hsx, mul_neg]
  have hmem : ∀ g : G ℚ Ω, g ∈ MulAction.stabilizer (G ℚ Ω) (δ * x) ↔ g (δ * x) = δ * x := fun g => Iff.rfl
  have : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
  refine ⟨MulAction.stabilizer (G ℚ Ω) (δ * x), stabilizer_isOpen_of_isIntegral _, ?_, fun τ hτ => complex_conjugation_fixes_sqrt hτ hpos hz,
    fun B' hB' => ?_, A, etaleSimplicialCochain_scheme_section_extension_naturality A hA (Nat.coprime_of_lt_prime hM (by omega) hp), fun h => hne (hsz.symm.trans (h hs))⟩
  · refine Subgroup.index_eq_two_iff.2 ⟨s, fun b => ?_⟩
    rw [hmem, hmem, AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hsz, map_neg]
    rcases galois_sqrt_sign b (n := (D * p : ℕ)) (hz.trans (Int.cast_natCast _).symm) with h | h
    · rw [h]; exact Or.inr ⟨rfl, hne⟩
    · rw [h, neg_neg]; exact Or.inl ⟨rfl, hne⟩
  · have hk : B'.valuation ((M * t : ℤ) : Ω) < 1 := by
      rw [Int.cast_mul, Int.cast_natCast, map_mul]
      exact mul_lt_one_of_lt_of_le hB' ((B'.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (intCast_mem B' t))
    ext g
    refine and_congr_right fun hg => ?_
    have hgx : g x = x := decomposition_fixes_sqrt B' (by rw [← ht', hx]; simp) hk hg
    show g (δ * x) = δ * x ↔ η g = 1
    rw [map_mul, hgx, hη]
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with h | h
    · simp [h]
    · rw [h]
      refine iff_of_false (fun h' => hne ?_) (by decide)
      simpa using h'


[MFC_SRC_HLAY] Full cyclotomic layer and inertia properties
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/HlayPlace.lean#L1-L132
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.Places
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.ContTw

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem hlay_place (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (p : Ω) < 1 := by
  have hmem : (p : Ω) ∈ (⊥ : Subring Ω) := Subring.mem_bot.mpr ⟨p, by simp⟩
  set x : (⊥ : Subring Ω) := ⟨p, hmem⟩
  have hI : Ideal.span {x} ≠ ⊤ := by
    rw [Ne, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_top]
    rintro ⟨u, hu⟩
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := Subring.mem_bot.mp (u⁻¹ : (⊥ : Subring Ω)ˣ).1.2
    have h1 : (u : (⊥ : Subring Ω)) * (↑u⁻¹ : (⊥ : Subring Ω)) = 1 := u.mul_inv
    have h2 : ((p : ℤ) * b : ℤ) = (1 : Ω) := by
      have := congrArg Subtype.val h1
      simp only [Subring.coe_mul, Subring.coe_one] at this
      rw [hu] at this
      push_cast
      rw [hb]
      exact this
    have h3 : (p : ℤ) * b = 1 := by exact_mod_cast h2
    have h4 : (p : ℤ) ∣ 1 := ⟨b, h3.symm⟩
    have : p ∣ 1 := by exact_mod_cast h4
    exact hp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.mp this)
  obtain ⟨B, -, hB⟩ := Ideal.image_subset_nonunits_valuationSubring _ hI
  exact ⟨B, (ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff B).mp (hB ⟨x, Ideal.subset_span rfl, rfl⟩)⟩

private theorem hlay_cop (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (m : ℕ) : (l ^ m).Coprime (l - 1) := Nat.Coprime.pow_left m ((Nat.coprime_self_sub_right hl.out.one_le).mpr (Nat.coprime_one_right _))

private theorem hlay_card (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (m : ℕ) (h2 : l ≠ 2) :
    Nat.card ((⊤ : Subgroup (ZMod (l ^ (m + 1)))ˣ).map (powMonoidHom (l - 1))) = l ^ m := by
  haveI := ZMod.isCyclic_units_of_prime_pow l hl.out h2 (m + 1)
  rw [← MonoidHom.range_eq_map, IsCyclic.card_powMonoidHom_range, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient,
    Nat.totient_prime_pow_succ hl.out]
  have hpos : 0 < l - 1 := Nat.sub_pos_of_lt hl.out.one_lt
  rw [Nat.gcd_comm, Nat.gcd_mul_left_right]
  exact Nat.mul_div_cancel _ hpos

private theorem hlay_inf {l : ℕ} [hl : Fact l.Prime] (m : ℕ) (u : ℤ_[l]ˣ) (h1 : u ∈ KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ l 1)
    (h : KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow l (m + 1) u ^ (l - 1) = 1) : KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow l (m + 1) u = 1 := by
  set x := KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow l (m + 1) u with hx
  have hd : l ^ 1 ∣ l ^ (m + 1) := pow_dvd_pow l (by omega)
  set f := ZMod.unitsMap hd
  have hfx : f x = 1 := by
    have : f x = KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow l 1 u := by
      ext
      simp only [f, hx, ZMod.unitsMap, KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe]
      exact congrArg (fun g => g (u : ℤ_[l])) (PadicInt.zmod_cast_comp_toZModPow 1 (m + 1) (by omega))
    rw [this]
    exact h1
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective f := ZMod.unitsMap_surjective hd
  have hcardG : Nat.card (ZMod (l ^ (m + 1)))ˣ = l ^ m * (l - 1) := by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, Nat.totient_prime_pow_succ hl.out]
  have hcardH : Nat.card (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ = l - 1 := by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, pow_one, Nat.totient_prime hl.out]
  have hker : Nat.card f.ker = l ^ m := by
    have e := Subgroup.card_mul_index f.ker
    rw [Subgroup.index_ker, MonoidHom.range_eq_top.mpr hsurj, Subgroup.card_top, hcardH, hcardG] at e
    exact Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_right (Nat.sub_pos_of_lt hl.out.one_lt) e
  have hpow : x ^ (l ^ m) = 1 := by
    have := pow_card_eq_one' (G := f.ker) (x := ⟨x, hfx⟩)
    rw [hker] at this
    exact congrArg Subtype.val this
  have hdvd : orderOf x ∣ Nat.gcd (l ^ m) (l - 1) :=
    Nat.dvd_gcd (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hpow) (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one h)
  rw [hlay_cop l m, Nat.dvd_one, orderOf_eq_one_iff] at hdvd
  exact hdvd

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.hlay :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (m : ℕ), l ≠ 2 → IsOpen (Hlay Ω l m : Set (G ℚ Ω)) ∧ commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hlay Ω l m ∧ (Hlay Ω l m).index = l ^ m ∧ Hlay Ω l m ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker = (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)).ker ∧ (∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hlay Ω l m) ∧ ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → (Hlay Ω l m).relIndex (Ine (F := ℚ) B') = l ^ m ∧ ∀ r, (Ine (F := ℚ) B').map (cycMod ℚ Ω l r) = ⊤ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ m h2
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := hlay_place Ω l Fact.out
  have hsurjIne : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
      ∀ r, (Ine (F := ℚ) B').map (cycMod ℚ Ω l r) = ⊤ := by
    intro B' hB' r
    rw [eq_top_iff]
    rintro y -
    obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow_surjective (l := l) r y
    obtain ⟨σ, hσ, hσv⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω l B' hB' v
    exact ⟨σ, hσ, by rw [← hσv]; rfl⟩
  have hidx : ∀ K : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), K.map (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)) = ⊤ →
      (Hlay Ω l m).relIndex K = l ^ m := by
    intro K hK
    have hsub : (Hlay Ω l m).subgroupOf K = ((cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)).comp K.subtype).ker := by
      ext x
      rfl
    rw [Subgroup.relIndex, hsub, Subgroup.index_ker]
    have hr : ((cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)).comp K.subtype).range =
        (⊤ : Subgroup (ZMod (l ^ (m + 1)))ˣ).map (powMonoidHom (l - 1)) := by
      ext y
      simp only [MonoidHom.mem_range, Subgroup.mem_map, Subgroup.mem_top, true_and, MonoidHom.comp_apply,
        Subgroup.coe_subtype, MonoidHom.pow_apply, powMonoidHom_apply]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨k, rfl⟩
        exact ⟨_, rfl⟩
      · rintro ⟨z, rfl⟩
        have hz : z ∈ K.map (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)) := hK ▸ Subgroup.mem_top z
        obtain ⟨k, hk, rfl⟩ := hz
        exact ⟨⟨k, hk⟩, rfl⟩
    rw [hr]
    exact hlay_card l m h2
  have hcont := (UpperTheory.V.Base.contTw ℚ Ω l).1
  have hkerle : (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)).ker ≤ Hlay Ω l m := by
    intro σ hσ
    show (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) σ) ^ (l - 1) = 1
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp hσ, one_pow]
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, fun B' hB' => ⟨hidx _ (hsurjIne B' hB' _), hsurjIne B' hB'⟩⟩
  · apply Subgroup.isOpen_mono hkerle
    have hset : ((cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)).ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) =
        cycZ ℚ Ω l ⁻¹' (KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ l (m + 1) : Set ℤ_[l]ˣ) := by
      ext σ
      rfl
    rw [hset]
    exact (KStar.TotallyRealRoots.isOpen_princ (m + 1)).preimage hcont
  · exact Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _
  · rw [← Subgroup.relIndex_top_right]
    exact hidx ⊤ (top_unique ((hsurjIne A hA _).symm.le.trans (Subgroup.map_mono le_top)))
  · apply le_antisymm
    · rintro σ ⟨h1, h1'⟩
      exact hlay_inf m (cycZ ℚ Ω l σ) h1' h1
    · intro σ hσ
      exact ⟨hkerle hσ, KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ_anti (by omega) hσ⟩
  · intro B' hB'
    refine ((UpperTheory.V.Base.places ℚ Ω l B').2.2.2 hB').trans ?_
    intro σ hσ
    apply hkerle
    show KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow l (m + 1) (cycZ ℚ Ω l σ) = 1
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp hσ, map_one]


[MFC_SRC_HTH] Exact Hth local and index properties; inherited CAF4
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/HthPlace.lean#L1-L111
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.Places
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.ContTw
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.GaloisCohomologyLength.LevelLeIne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.CyclotomicBounds.ClassNumberBounds.TotallyRealRoots.PrincTop

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem hth_place (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (p : Ω) < 1 := by
  obtain ⟨B, -, hB⟩ := Ideal.image_subset_nonunits_valuationSubring
    (Ideal.span {(⟨(p : Ω), natCast_mem _ p⟩ : (⊥ : Subring Ω))}) (by
      intro h
      rw [Ideal.span_singleton_eq_top] at h
      obtain ⟨⟨b, hb⟩, hb1⟩ := isUnit_iff_exists_inv.mp h
      obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ := Subring.mem_bot.mp hb
      have h0 : ((p : Ω) * (n : Ω)) = 1 := congrArg Subtype.val hb1
      have h1 : ((p : ℤ) * n : ℤ) = 1 := by exact_mod_cast h0
      have h2 : (p : ℤ) ∣ 1 := ⟨n, h1.symm⟩
      have h3 : p ∣ 1 := by exact_mod_cast h2
      exact hp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.mp h3))
  exact ⟨B, B.mem_nonunits_iff.mp (hB ⟨_, Ideal.subset_span rfl, rfl⟩)⟩

private theorem hth_spec (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (w : ℕ) [hw : Fact w.Prime]
    (s : G ℚ Ω) (ζ : Ω) (h : ζ ^ w = 1) : s ζ = ζ ^ ((cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have h1 : ζ ^ w ^ 1 = 1 := by rwa [pow_one]
  exact cyclotomicCharacter.spec (L := Ω) w (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G ℚ Ω) Ω s) ζ h1

private theorem hth_iff (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime]
    (w : ℕ) [hw : Fact w.Prime] (s : G ℚ Ω) :
    (∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l) ↔
      cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s ∈ (powMonoidHom l : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ →* (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ).range := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : NeZero w := ⟨hw.out.ne_zero⟩
  have hred : ∀ (ζ : Ω) (m : ℕ), ζ ^ w = 1 → ζ ^ m = ζ ^ (m % w) := by
    intro ζ m hζ
    conv_lhs => rw [← Nat.mod_add_div m w, pow_add, pow_mul, hζ, one_pow, mul_one]
  have hcv : ((cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val < w :=
    lt_of_lt_of_eq (ZMod.val_lt _) (pow_one w)
  have hl0 : l ≠ 0 := hl.out.ne_zero
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨a, ha⟩
    obtain ⟨ζ0, hζ0⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω w
    have e1 := ha ζ0 hζ0.pow_eq_one
    rw [hth_spec Ω w s ζ0 hζ0.pow_eq_one, hred ζ0 (a ^ l) hζ0.pow_eq_one] at e1
    have e2 : ((cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val = a ^ l % w :=
      hζ0.pow_inj hcv (Nat.mod_lt _ (Nat.pos_of_neZero w)) e1
    have e3 : ((cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)) = (a : ZMod (w ^ 1)) ^ l := by
      apply ZMod.val_injective
      rw [e2, ← Nat.cast_pow, ZMod.val_natCast, pow_one]
    have hu : IsUnit (a : ZMod (w ^ 1)) := (isUnit_pow_iff hl0).mp (e3 ▸ (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s).isUnit)
    exact ⟨hu.unit, Units.ext (by simp [e3])⟩
  · rintro ⟨u, hu⟩
    refine ⟨(u : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val, fun ζ hζ => ?_⟩
    have e : ((cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)) =
        (((u : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val ^ l : ℕ) : ZMod (w ^ 1)) := by
      rw [← hu]
      simp
    rw [hth_spec Ω w s ζ hζ, hred ζ (((u : ZMod (w ^ 1)).val) ^ l) hζ, e, hred ζ _ hζ,
      ZMod.val_natCast, Nat.mod_mod_of_dvd _ (dvd_pow_self w one_ne_zero)]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.hth :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (w : ℕ), w.Prime → w ≡ 1 [MOD l] → IsOpen (Hth Ω l w : Set (G ℚ Ω)) ∧ commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hth Ω l w ∧ (Hth Ω l w).index = l ∧ (∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hth Ω l w) ∧ ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 → ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hth Ω l w := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl w hwp hw1
  haveI hw : Fact w.Prime := ⟨hwp⟩
  haveI : Fact (w ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rw [pow_one]; exact hwp⟩
  have hmem : ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s ∈ Hth Ω l w ↔
      cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s ∈ (powMonoidHom l : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ →* (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ).range := by
    intro s
    change (∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l) ∧
      (∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l) ↔ _
    rw [hth_iff Ω l w s, hth_iff Ω l w s⁻¹, map_inv, inv_mem_iff, and_self]
  have hker : (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1).ker ≤ Hth Ω l w := fun s hs =>
    (hmem s).mpr (by rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp hs]; exact one_mem _)
  have hZ : ∀ s, cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s = KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow w 1 (cycZ ℚ Ω w s) := fun s => rfl
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := hth_place Ω w hwp
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1) := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨u, rfl⟩ := KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow_surjective (l := w) 1 v
    obtain ⟨σ, -, hσ⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω w A hA u
    exact ⟨σ, by rw [hZ, hσ]⟩
  have hidx : (Hth Ω l w).index = l := by
    have hH : Hth Ω l w =
        (powMonoidHom l : (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ →* (ZMod (w ^ 1))ˣ).range.comap (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1) := by
      ext s
      exact hmem s
    rw [hH, Subgroup.index_comap_of_surjective _ hsurj, IsCyclic.index_powMonoidHom_range,
      Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card_units_eq_totient, pow_one, Nat.totient_prime hwp]
    exact Nat.gcd_eq_right ((Nat.modEq_iff_dvd' hwp.one_lt.le).mp hw1.symm)
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, hidx, ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := (KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ w 1).comap (cycZ ℚ Ω w)) ?_ ?_
    · intro s hs
      apply hker
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hZ]
      exact hs
    · exact (contTw ℚ Ω w).1.isOpen_preimage _ (KStar.TotallyRealRoots.isOpen_princ 1)
  · exact (Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1)).trans hker
  · intro B' hB' s hs
    apply hker
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hZ, MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp ((places ℚ Ω w B').2.2.2 hB' hs), map_one]
  · intro B' hB' hle
    have htop : Hth Ω l w = ⊤ := by
      refine eq_top_iff.mpr fun s _ => ?_
      obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := KStar.TotallyRealRoots.redPow_surjective (l := w) 1 (cycMod ℚ Ω w 1 s)
      obtain ⟨σ, hσI, hσ⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω w B' hB' u
      have h1 := (hmem σ).mp (hle hσI)
      rw [hZ, hσ, hu] at h1
      exact (hmem s).mpr h1
    rw [htop, Subgroup.index_top] at hidx
    exact hl.out.one_lt.ne hidx


[MFC_SRC_L15] Residual identity persistence l15 and power absorption
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/RootOfUnityEqOneOfCloseResidue.lean#L1-L146
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.Places

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem aux_one {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) {m : ℕ} (hm : m ≠ 0) {ζ : Ω}
    (h : ζ ^ m = 1) : A.valuation ζ = 1 := by
  have h1 : A.valuation ζ ^ m = 1 := by rw [← map_pow, h, map_one]
  exact (pow_eq_one_iff_left hm).1 h1

private theorem vg_ne {Γ : Type} [LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ] : (0 : Γ) < 1 := by
  refine lt_of_le_of_ne zero_le' fun h => ?_
  obtain ⟨x, y, hxy⟩ := (GroupWithZero.toNontrivial (G₀ := Γ)).exists_pair_ne
  exact hxy (by rw [← mul_one x, ← mul_one y, ← h, mul_zero, mul_zero])

private theorem power_bounds_of_between {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) {η : Ω} (h1 : A.valuation η = 1)
    (h : A.valuation (η - 1) < 1) (i : ℕ) : A.valuation (1 - η ^ i) < 1 := by
  induction i with
  | zero => rw [pow_zero, sub_self, map_zero]; exact vg_ne
  | succ i ih =>
    have e : 1 - η ^ (i + 1) = η * (1 - η ^ i) - (η - 1) := by ring
    rw [e]
    refine A.valuation.map_sub_lt ?_ h
    rw [map_mul, h1, one_mul]; exact ih

private theorem root_of_unity_eq_one_of_close_residue {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) {m : ℕ} (hm : m ≠ 0)
    (hv : ¬ A.valuation (m : Ω) < 1) {η : Ω} (hη : η ^ m = 1) (h : A.valuation (η - 1) < 1) : η = 1 := by
  by_contra hne
  apply hv
  have hs : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, η ^ i = 0 := by
    have := mul_geom_sum η m
    rw [hη, sub_self] at this
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_left (sub_ne_zero.2 hne)
  have e : (m : Ω) = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, (1 - η ^ i) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, hs]; simp
  rw [e]
  have h10 : (1 : A.ValueGroup) ≠ 0 := vg_ne.ne'
  exact A.valuation.map_sum_lt h10 fun i _ => power_bounds_of_between A (aux_one A hm hη) h i

private theorem ine_fix {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) {m : ℕ} (hm : m ≠ 0)
    (hv : ¬ A.valuation (m : Ω) < 1) {g : G ℚ Ω} (hg : g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) (ζ : Ω) (hζ : ζ ^ m = 1) : g ζ = ζ := by
  have h1 := aux_one A hm hζ
  have h0 : ζ ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    rw [zero_pow hm] at hζ
    exact zero_ne_one hζ
  have e : g ζ / ζ - 1 = (g ζ - ζ) / ζ := by field_simp
  have : g ζ / ζ = 1 := by
    refine root_of_unity_eq_one_of_close_residue A hm hv (by rw [div_pow, ← map_pow, hζ, map_one, div_one]) ?_
    rw [e, map_div₀, h1, div_one]; exact hg.2 ζ h1.le
  exact (div_eq_one_iff_eq h0).1 this

private theorem val_nat_le {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (n : ℕ) : A.valuation (n : Ω) ≤ 1 :=
  (A.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (natCast_mem A n)

private theorem act_pow {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {w : ℕ} (hw : w ≠ 0) (n : ℕ) (s : G ℚ Ω) :
    ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → (s ^ n) ζ = ζ ^ a ^ n := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : NeZero w := ⟨hw⟩
  haveI : NeZero (w : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hw⟩
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω w
  obtain ⟨a, -, ha⟩ := hz.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one (ξ := s z) (by rw [← map_pow, hz.pow_eq_one, map_one])
  have h1 : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a := fun ζ h => by
    obtain ⟨b, -, rfl⟩ := hz.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one h
    rw [map_pow, ← ha, ← pow_mul, ← pow_mul, mul_comm]
  refine ⟨a, fun ζ h => ?_⟩
  induction n with
  | zero => simp
  | succ n ih => rw [pow_succ', AlgEquiv.mul_apply, ih, map_pow, h1 ζ h, ← pow_mul, pow_succ']

private theorem pow_mem_Upl {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    {Hb H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)} {w : ℕ} (hw : w ≠ 0) (hab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hb) (hidx : ∃ a : ℕ, Hb.index = l ^ a) (m : ℕ) :
    ∃ N : ℕ, ∀ s ∈ H₀, s ^ l ^ N ∈ Upl l Hb H₀ w m := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hidx
  haveI : Hb.Normal := ⟨fun n hn g => by
    have : g * n * g⁻¹ = g * n * g⁻¹ * n⁻¹ * n := by group
    rw [this]
    exact Hb.mul_mem (hab (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top g) (Subgroup.mem_top n))) hn⟩
  refine ⟨1 + a + m, fun s hs => ⟨⟨⟨H₀.pow_mem hs _, ?_⟩, ?_⟩, ?_⟩⟩
  · have : s ^ l ∈ Hth Ω l w := ⟨act_pow hw l s, by
      show ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → (s ^ l)⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l
      rw [← inv_pow]; exact act_pow hw l s⁻¹⟩
    rw [add_assoc, pow_add, pow_one, pow_mul]
    exact Subgroup.pow_mem _ this _
  · have : s ^ l ^ a ∈ Hb := ha ▸ Hb.pow_index_mem s
    rw [add_comm 1 a, add_assoc, pow_add, pow_mul]
    exact Subgroup.pow_mem _ this _
  · have : s ^ l ^ m ∈ Hlay Ω l m := by
      have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
      haveI : NeZero (l ^ (m + 1)) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.ne_zero⟩
      show (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)) (s ^ l ^ m) = 1
      rw [MonoidHom.pow_apply, map_pow, ← pow_mul, ← Nat.totient_prime_pow_succ hl, ← ZMod.card_units_eq_totient]
      exact pow_card_eq_one
    rw [add_comm (1 + a) m, pow_add, pow_mul]
    exact Subgroup.pow_mem _ this _

private theorem core {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [Field k] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] [CharP k l] (κ : Γ →* kˣ)
    (U H₀ E : Subgroup Γ) (hU : ∃ N : ℕ, ∀ s ∈ H₀, s ^ l ^ N ∈ U) (h2 : H₀.index = 2) (t : Γ) (ht : t ∉ H₀) (htE : t ∈ E)
    (hκ : κ t = 1) (h : ∀ s ∈ U ⊓ E, κ s = 1) : ∀ s ∈ E, κ s = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := hU
  have h0 : ∀ s ∈ H₀, s ∈ E → κ s = 1 := fun s hs hE => by
    have h1 := h _ ⟨hN s hs, E.pow_mem hE _⟩
    rw [map_pow] at h1
    have h3 : (κ s : k) ^ l ^ N = 1 := by rw [← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, h1, Units.val_one]
    have h4 : ((κ s : k) - 1) ^ l ^ N = 0 := by rw [sub_pow_char_pow, h3, one_pow, sub_self]
    exact Units.ext (sub_eq_zero.1 (pow_eq_zero_iff (pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero) |>.1 h4))
  intro s hs
  by_cases hH : s ∈ H₀
  · exact h0 s hH hs
  · have := h0 (s * t) ((Subgroup.mul_mem_iff_of_index_two h2).2 (by simp [hH, ht])) (E.mul_mem hs htE)
    rwa [map_mul, hκ, mul_one] at this

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.l15 :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w : ℕ), FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum η M H₀ → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → ∀ (m : ℕ) (i j : ℤ), ((∀ s ∈ Upl l Hb H₀ w m, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = 1) → ∀ s, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = 1) ∧ ((∀ s ∈ Upl l Hb H₀ w m, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = etaO O η s) → ∀ s, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = etaO O η s) ∧ ((∀ s ∈ Upl l Hb H₀ w m ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = 1) → ∀ s ∈ η.ker, omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j = 1) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW m i j
  have hU := pow_mem_Upl l (H₀ := H₀) hW.prime.ne_zero hG.Hb_ab hG.Hb_idx m
  obtain ⟨A, hA, hI⟩ := h0.ram
  obtain ⟨t, ht, htH⟩ := SetLike.not_le_iff_exists.1 hI
  obtain ⟨a, ka, kb⟩ := hG.cyc
  have hM1 : A.valuation (M : Ω) = 1 := le_antisymm (val_nat_le A M) (not_lt.1 hA)
  have hMa : ¬ A.valuation ((M ^ a : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
    rw [Nat.cast_pow, map_pow, hM1, one_pow]; exact lt_irrefl _
  have hfix := ine_fix A (pow_ne_zero a hG.M_ne) hMa ht
  have hη : η t = 1 := ka t hfix
  have hΨ : barChar O Ψ t = 1 := kb t hfix
  have hlv : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun h => hA (by
    obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    rw [Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' (val_nat_le A c)) h)
  have hω : omO Ω l O t = 1 := by
    have h1 : cycZ ℚ Ω l t = 1 := (UpperTheory.V.Base.places ℚ Ω l A).2.2.2 hlv ht
    simp [GC.cycF, cycMod, h1]
  have he : etaO O η t = 1 := by simp [hη]
  let κ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := omO Ω l O ^ i * barChar O Ψ ^ j
  have hκ : ∀ s, κ s = omO Ω l O s ^ i * barChar O Ψ s ^ j := fun s => by simp [κ]
  have hκt : κ t = 1 := by rw [hκ, hω, hΨ]; simp
  refine ⟨fun h s => ?_, fun h s => ?_, fun h s hs => ?_⟩
  · rw [← hκ]
    exact core l κ _ H₀ ⊤ hU h0.idx t htH trivial hκt (fun s hs => by rw [hκ]; exact h s hs.1) s trivial
  · have := core l (κ * (etaO O η)⁻¹) _ H₀ ⊤ hU h0.idx t htH trivial
      (by rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, hκt, he]; simp)
      (fun s hs => by rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, hκ, h s hs.1, mul_inv_cancel]) s trivial
    rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, hκ] at this
    exact mul_inv_eq_one.1 this
  · rw [← hκ]
    exact core l κ _ H₀ η.ker hU h0.idx t htH hη hκt (fun s hs => by rw [hκ]; exact h s hs) s hs


[MFC_SRC_UFACTS] Full original ufacts record

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.ufacts :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w : ℕ), FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum η M H₀ → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → ∀ m, UFacts l η Ψ M m (Upl l Hb H₀ w m) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW m
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨thO, thA, thI, thU, thR⟩ := hth Ω l w hW.prime hW.one
  obtain ⟨lyO, lyA, lyI, lyK, lyU, lyR⟩ := hlay Ω l m hG.odd
  obtain ⟨pb1, pb2, pb3, pb4, pb5, pb6, pb7, pb8, pb9, pb10⟩ := ML.placeBasic Ω
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hG.Hb_idx
  have hU : Upl l Hb H₀ w m = H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m := rfl
  have hoddpow : ∀ (H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (b : ℕ), commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ H → H.index = l ^ b →
      ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, τ ^ 2 = 1 → τ ∈ H := by
    intro H b hc hi τ hτ
    have h1 := uf_pow hc hi le_rfl τ
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ : Odd (l ^ b) := (hl.odd_of_ne_two hG.odd).pow
    rw [hk, pow_succ, pow_mul, hτ, one_pow, one_mul] at h1
    exact h1
  have hMl : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := by
    intro B' hB'
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    have := (pb3 B' l c).2 (Or.inl hB')
    simpa [ML.Over, hc] using this
  have hσom : omO Ω l O σ = -1 := uf_om l (uf_cc l hG.cc)
  have hσb := hG.even
  have hneg : (-1 : (ResidueField O)ˣ) ≠ 1 := uf_neg l hG.odd
  obtain ⟨s', hs'η, hs'om, hs'b⟩ : ∃ s', η s' = 1 ∧ omO Ω l O s' = -1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s' = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨t, ⟨ht1, ht2⟩, ht3⟩ := SetLike.not_le_iff_exists.1 hG.g4
    by_cases hησ : η σ = 1
    · exact ⟨σ, hησ, hσom, hσb⟩
    · refine ⟨t * σ, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
      · have e1 := (Int.units_eq_one_or (η t)).resolve_left ht3
        have e2 := (Int.units_eq_one_or (η σ)).resolve_left hησ
        rw [map_mul, e1, e2]
        rfl
      · rw [map_mul, MonoidHom.mem_ker.1 ht1, hσom, one_mul]
      · rw [map_mul, MonoidHom.mem_ker.1 ht2, hσb, one_mul]
  rw [hU]
  refine ⟨?isOpen, ?ab, ?real, ?unr, ?split, ?teich, ?mu, ?deg, ?f2b, ?f2c, ?f3, ?f5⟩
  case isOpen =>
    simp only [Subgroup.coe_inf]
    exact ((h0.isOpen.inter thO).inter hG.Hb_open).inter lyO
  case ab => exact le_inf (le_inf (le_inf (uf_comm h0.idx) thA) hG.Hb_ab) lyA
  case real =>
    intro τ hτ
    have h2 := uf_sq hτ
    exact ⟨⟨⟨h0.real τ hτ, hoddpow _ 1 thA (by rw [thI, pow_one]) τ h2⟩, hoddpow _ a hG.Hb_ab ha τ h2⟩,
      hoddpow _ m lyA lyI τ h2⟩
  case unr =>
    intro B' s hs
    by_cases hB' : B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1
    · have h1 : s ∈ Dec B' ⊓ H₀ := ⟨Ine_le_Dec B' hs.1, hs.2.1.1.1⟩
      rw [h0.loc B' hB'] at h1
      exact h1.2
    · obtain ⟨c, hc1, -⟩ := hG.cyc
      have hno : ∀ e : ℕ, ¬ ML.Over B' (M ^ e) := by
        intro e
        induction e with
        | zero => simp [ML.Over]
        | succ e ih =>
          rw [pow_succ, pb3]
          rintro (h | h)
          · exact ih h
          · exact hB' h
      exact MonoidHom.mem_ker.2 (hc1 s fun ζ hζ => pb5 B' (M ^ c) (hno c) hs.1 ζ hζ)
  case split =>
    intro B' hB' s hs
    have h1 : s ∈ Dec B' ⊓ H₀ := ⟨hs.1, hs.2.1.1.1⟩
    rw [h0.loc B' hB'] at h1
    exact h1.2
  case teich =>
    intro B' hB' s hs hb
    exact hG.teich B' hB' s ⟨hs.1, hs.2.1.2⟩ hb
  case mu =>
    intro B' hB'
    have hm1 : (-1 : (ZMod (l ^ (m + 1)))ˣ) ∈ Subgroup.map (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)) (Ine B') := by
      rw [(lyR B' hB').2 (m + 1)]
      trivial
    obtain ⟨s, hsI, hs⟩ := Subgroup.mem_map.1 hm1
    have hsLay : s ∈ Hlay Ω l m := by
      show (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)) s = 1
      rw [MonoidHom.pow_apply, hs]
      exact (hl.even_sub_one hG.odd).neg_one_pow
    have hsH0 : s ∈ H₀ := by
      have h1 : s ∈ Dec B' ⊓ η.ker := ⟨Ine_le_Dec B' hsI, hG.η_l B' hB' hsI⟩
      rw [← h0.loc B' (hMl B' hB')] at h1
      exact h1.2
    have hlw : l ≠ w := by
      intro hlw
      have h1 := hW.one
      rw [← hlw] at h1
      unfold Nat.ModEq at h1
      rw [Nat.mod_self, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt hl.one_lt] at h1
      exact zero_ne_one h1
    have hsTh : s ∈ Hth Ω l w :=
      thU B' (pb4 B' l w ((Nat.coprime_primes hl hW.prime).2 hlw) hB') hsI
    refine ⟨s, ⟨hsI, ⟨⟨⟨hsH0, hsTh⟩, hG.Hb_l B' hB' hsI⟩, hsLay⟩⟩, fun h1 => ?_⟩
    have h2 : s ∈ Hlay Ω l m ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker := ⟨hsLay, h1⟩
    rw [lyK, MonoidHom.mem_ker, hs] at h2
    haveI : Fact (2 < l ^ (m + 1)) := ⟨by
      calc 2 < l := lt_of_le_of_ne hl.two_le (Ne.symm hG.odd)
        _ = l ^ 1 := (pow_one l).symm
        _ ≤ l ^ (m + 1) := Nat.pow_le_pow_right hl.pos (by omega)⟩
    exact ZMod.neg_one_ne_one (congrArg Units.val h2)
  case deg =>
    intro B' hB'
    have hfi : (H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m).index ≠ 0 := by
      refine Subgroup.index_inf_ne_zero (Subgroup.index_inf_ne_zero (Subgroup.index_inf_ne_zero ?_ ?_) ?_) ?_
      · rw [h0.idx]; norm_num
      · rw [thI]; exact hl.ne_zero
      · rw [ha]; exact pow_ne_zero _ hl.ne_zero
      · rw [lyI]; exact pow_ne_zero _ hl.ne_zero
    have hrel : ∀ H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), H.index ≠ 0 → ∀ L : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), H.relIndex L ≠ 0 :=
      fun H hH L => Subgroup.relIndex_ne_zero_trans (K := ⊤) (by rw [Subgroup.relIndex_top_right]; exact hH)
        (by rw [Subgroup.relIndex_top_left]; exact one_ne_zero)
    have hlayi : (Hlay Ω l m).index ≠ 0 := by rw [lyI]; exact pow_ne_zero _ hl.ne_zero
    calc l ^ m = (Hlay Ω l m).relIndex (Ine B') := ((lyR B' hB').1).symm
      _ ≤ (Hlay Ω l m).relIndex (Dec B') :=
        Subgroup.relIndex_le_of_le_right (Ine_le_Dec B') (hrel _ hlayi _)
      _ ≤ (H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m).relIndex (Dec B') :=
        Subgroup.relIndex_le_of_le_left inf_le_right (hrel _ hfi _)
  case f2b =>
    intro j _
    by_contra hc
    push_neg at hc
    have h1 := (l15 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW m 1 j).2.2 (fun s hs => by rw [zpow_one]; exact hc s hs) s'
      (MonoidHom.mem_ker.2 hs'η)
    rw [zpow_one, hs'om, hs'b, one_zpow, mul_one] at h1
    exact hneg h1
  case f2c =>
    intro j _
    by_contra hc
    push_neg at hc
    have h1 := (l15 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW m 1 j).2.2 (fun s hs => by rw [zpow_one]; exact hc s hs) s'
      (MonoidHom.mem_ker.2 hs'η)
    rw [zpow_one, hs'om, hs'b, one_zpow, mul_one] at h1
    exact hneg h1
  case f3 =>
    intro B' hB'
    obtain ⟨t, htD, htη, htb⟩ := hG.g5
    obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := pb8 B B' l hl hG.over_l hB'
    have ht'D : g * t * g⁻¹ ∈ Dec B' := by
      rw [hg, (pb10 B g).1]
      convert htD using 1
      group
    have ht'η : η (g * t * g⁻¹) = 1 := by
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, htη, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel]
    have ht'b : barChar O Ψ (g * t * g⁻¹) = barChar O Ψ t := by
      rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm (barChar O Ψ g), mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel, mul_one]
    have ht'H0 : g * t * g⁻¹ ∈ H₀ := by
      have h1 : g * t * g⁻¹ ∈ Dec B' ⊓ η.ker := ⟨ht'D, ht'η⟩
      rw [← h0.loc B' (hMl B' hB')] at h1
      exact h1.2
    refine ⟨(g * t * g⁻¹) ^ l ^ (a + m + 1), ⟨pow_mem ht'D _, ⟨⟨⟨pow_mem ht'H0 _,
      uf_pow thA (by rw [thI, pow_one]) (by omega) _⟩, uf_pow hG.Hb_ab ha (by omega) _⟩,
      uf_pow lyA lyI (by omega) _⟩⟩, fun h1 => htb ?_⟩
    rw [← ht'b]
    apply uf_frob l (a := a + m + 1)
    rw [map_pow, ← pow_mul, mul_comm, pow_mul] at h1
    exact h1
  case f5 =>
    rintro ⟨j, hj⟩
    have h1 := (l15 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW m 1 (-j)).2.2
      (fun s hs => by rw [zpow_one, hj s hs, ← zpow_add, add_neg_cancel, zpow_zero]) s' (MonoidHom.mem_ker.2 hs'η)
    rw [zpow_one, hs'om, hs'b, one_zpow, mul_one] at h1
    exact hneg h1
