private theorem rank_le_of_cardLe (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (P : Type) [AddCommGroup P] [Module (ZMod l) P] (r : ℕ)
    (h : Two.CardLe P (l ^ r)) : Module.rank (ZMod l) P ≤ r := by
  obtain ⟨hfin, hcard⟩ := h
  haveI := hfin
  haveI : Module.Finite (ZMod l) P := Module.Finite.of_finite
  rw [← Module.finrank_eq_rank]
  have hc : Nat.card P = l ^ Module.finrank (ZMod l) P := by
    haveI := Fintype.ofFinite P
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Module.card_eq_pow_finrank (K := ZMod l), ZMod.card]
  rw [hc] at hcard
  have hle := (Nat.pow_le_pow_iff_right hl.out.one_lt).mp hcard
  exact_mod_cast hle

private theorem lev_rk {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (h2 : l ≠ 2)
    (V : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (ho : IsOpen (V : Set (G ℚ Ω))) (hab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ V) (hμ : V ≤ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) :
    ∃ r : ℕ, ∀ n, GC.clRk l (V ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l n).ker) ≤ r := by
  haveI : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  let H : ClosedSubgroup (G ℚ Ω) := ⟨V, Subgroup.isClosed_of_isOpen V ho⟩
  let E := IntermediateField.fixedField V
  have hEV : E.fixingSubgroup = V := InfiniteGalois.fixingSubgroup_fixedField H
  have hnorm : V.Normal := by
    constructor
    intro v hv g
    have hc : g * v * g⁻¹ * v⁻¹ ∈ V := hab (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top g) (Subgroup.mem_top v))
    have := V.mul_mem hc hv
    simpa using this
  haveI hfd : FiniteDimensional ℚ E := by
    rw [← InfiniteGalois.isOpen_iff_finite, hEV]
    exact ho
  have hG := (InfiniteGalois.normal_iff_isGalois E).mp (hEV ▸ hnorm)
  have hquot : ∀ a b : G ℚ Ω ⧸ V, a * b = b * a := by
    intro a b
    induction a using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
    | H x =>
      induction b using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
      | H y =>
        rw [← QuotientGroup.mk_mul, ← QuotientGroup.mk_mul, QuotientGroup.eq]
        have hc : y⁻¹ * x⁻¹ * y⁻¹⁻¹ * x⁻¹⁻¹ ∈ V :=
          hab (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top _) (Subgroup.mem_top _))
        simpa [mul_assoc] using hc
  haveI : H.Normal := hnorm
  have hc2 : ∀ x y : @AlgEquiv ℚ E E _ _ _ (IntermediateField.algebra' E) (IntermediateField.algebra' E),
      x * y = y * x := fun x y =>
    (InfiniteGalois.normalAutEquivQuotient H).symm.injective (by rw [map_mul, map_mul, hquot])
  have hA : (IntermediateField.algebra' E : Algebra ℚ E) = DivisionRing.toRatAlgebra := Subsingleton.elim _ _
  rw [hA] at hc2 hG
  haveI hAb : IsAbelianGalois ℚ E := @IsAbelianGalois.mk _ _ _ _ _ hG ⟨⟨hc2⟩⟩
  haveI : NumberField E := NumberField.mk
  have hgal : galOf ℚ E Ω = V := by
    rw [← hEV]
    ext σ
    rfl
  have hker : ∀ n, (cycMod ℚ Ω l n).ker = (Two.galChar Ω (l ^ n)).ker := by
    intro n
    haveI : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
    have h := (Two.levChar Ω l n ⊤).1
    rw [levU, KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ, MonoidHom.comap_ker, top_inf_eq, top_inf_eq] at h
    exact h
  haveI : NeZero l := ⟨hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  have hζ : ∃ ζ : E, IsPrimitiveRoot ζ l := by
    haveI : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
    have h1 : ∃ ζ : E, IsPrimitiveRoot ζ (l ^ 1) := by
      rw [Two.muIff Ω E (l ^ 1), hgal, ← hker 1]
      exact hμ
    simpa using h1
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
  haveI : Module.IsTorsionFree E Ω := DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree
  haveI : IsAlgClosure E Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) E⟩
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := FW.main l E (by rw [if_neg h2]; exact hζ)
  refine ⟨r, fun n => ?_⟩
  let K' := Kum.Fn E Ω l n
  obtain ⟨hcyc, -, -⟩ := Kum.fnCyc E Ω l n
  haveI := hcyc
  haveI : NumberField K' := IsCyclotomicExtension.numberField {l ^ n} E K'
  have hgal' : galOf ℚ K' Ω = V ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l n).ker := by
    rw [Kum.galOfCyc ℚ Ω l n E K', hgal, levU, KStar.TotallyRealRoots.princ, MonoidHom.comap_ker]
    rfl
  haveI : Module.IsTorsionFree K' Ω := DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree
  haveI : IsAlgClosure K' Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) K'⟩
  have h2z : ∀ m : ZMod l, 2 • m = 0 → m = 0 := by
    intro m hm
    have h2u : (2 : ZMod l) ≠ 0 := by
      intro h
      have h' : ((2 : ℕ) : ZMod l) = 0 := by exact_mod_cast h
      rw [ZMod.natCast_eq_zero_iff] at h'
      exact h2 ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).mp h')
    rw [nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat] at hm
    exact (mul_eq_zero.mp hm).resolve_left h2u
  have hmap := Kum.pullOnto ℚ Ω K' (V ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l n).ker) (le_of_eq hgal'.symm) 1 (ZMod l) (ZMod l)
    one_ne_zero (le_of_eq hgal') (Or.inr h2z)
  show Module.rank (ZMod l) (Kum.XDual 1 (V ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l n).ker) (ZMod l) (ZMod l)) ≤ r
  rw [← hmap]
  have hc := Two.cardClDual K' l 1
  calc _ ≤ _ := rank_map_le _ _
    _ ≤ r := rank_le_of_cardLe l _ r ⟨hc.1, hc.2.trans (hr n K')⟩

private theorem kills_open {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {C : Type} [Group C] (N : ℕ) (hN : N ≠ 0)
    (χ : G ℚ Ω →* C) (h : Af.KillsMu N χ) : IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
  classical
  let S : Finset Ω := (Polynomial.nthRoots N (1 : Ω)).toFinset
  have hS : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ N = 1 → ζ ∈ (S : Set Ω) := by
    intro ζ hζ
    simp only [S, Finset.mem_coe, Multiset.mem_toFinset]
    exact (Polynomial.mem_nthRoots (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN)).mpr hζ
  have hint : ∀ x ∈ (S : Set Ω), IsIntegral ℚ x := by
    intro x hx
    simp only [S, Finset.mem_coe, Multiset.mem_toFinset] at hx
    have hx' := (Polynomial.mem_nthRoots (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN)).mp hx
    apply IsIntegral.of_pow (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN)
    rw [hx']
    exact isIntegral_one
  let E := IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ (S : Set Ω)
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ E := IntermediateField.finiteDimensional_adjoin hint
  have hle : E.fixingSubgroup ≤ χ.ker := by
    intro s hs
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    apply h
    intro ζ hζ
    have hm : ζ ∈ E := IntermediateField.subset_adjoin ℚ _ (hS ζ hζ)
    exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff E s).mp hs ζ hm
  exact Subgroup.isOpen_mono hle (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen E)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.b0 :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w : ℕ), FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum η M H₀ → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → ∃ C₀ : ℕ, ∀ m, GC.clRk l (Upl l Hb H₀ w m ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hW
  obtain ⟨a, hηk, hΨk⟩ := hG.cyc
  have hMa : M ^ a ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero _ hG.M_ne
  have hth' := hth Ω l w hW.prime hW.one
  have hk1 : Af.KillsMu l (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1) := by
    haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
    intro s hs
    apply Units.ext
    rw [Units.val_one]
    show PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (cyclotomicCharacter Ω l (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G ℚ Ω) Ω s) : ℤ_[l]) = 1
    rw [cyclotomicCharacter.toZModPow]
    symm
    apply modularCyclotomicCharacter.unique
    intro t ht
    have htn : (t : Ω) ^ (l ^ 1) = 1 := by
      rw [mem_rootsOfUnity] at ht
      rw [← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ht, Units.val_one]
    change s (t : Ω) = (t : Ω) ^ (1 : ZMod (l ^ 1)).val
    have h1 : l ^ 1 ≠ 1 := by
      rw [pow_one]
      exact (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt.ne'
    rw [ZMod.val_one'' h1, pow_one]
    exact hs t (by simpa using htn)
  have hηo := kills_open (M ^ a) hMa η hηk
  have hΨo := kills_open (M ^ a) hMa (barChar O Ψ) hΨk
  have h1o := kills_open l (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero _ hk1
  have hc0 : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ H₀ := by
    rw [commutator_def, Subgroup.commutator_le]
    intro x _ y _
    rw [commutatorElement_def]
    simp only [Subgroup.mul_mem_iff_of_index_two h0.idx, inv_mem_iff]
    tauto
  set V := H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker with hV
  have hVo : IsOpen (V : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
    simp only [hV, Subgroup.coe_inf]
    exact ((((h0.isOpen.inter hth'.1).inter hG.Hb_open).inter hηo).inter hΨo).inter h1o
  have hVc : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ V :=
    le_inf (le_inf (le_inf (le_inf (le_inf hc0 hth'.2.1) hG.Hb_ab) (Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _))
      (Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _)) (Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _)
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := lev_rk l hG.odd V hVo hVc inf_le_right
  refine ⟨r, fun m => ?_⟩
  have heq : Upl l Hb H₀ w m ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker =
      V ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1)).ker := by
    rw [← (hlay Ω l m hG.odd).2.2.2.1]
    ext s
    simp only [hV, Upl, Subgroup.mem_inf]
    tauto
  rw [heq]
  exact hr (m + 1)


[MFC_SRC_XDUAL] Literal XDual inertia and decomposition conditions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/Mk0IdMapCongr.lean#L90-L237
def XDual (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (R M : Type) [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M] : Submodule R (U → M) where
  carrier := {φ | IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧
    (∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (u : U), u.1 ∈ Ine (F := F) A → φ u = 0) ∧
    ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → ∀ u : U, u.1 ∈ Dec (F := F) A → φ u = 0}
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const _, by simp, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨ha.1.add hb.1, fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.2.1, hb.2.1]; abel,
    fun A u h => by simp [ha.2.2.1 A u h, hb.2.2.1 A u h],
    fun A hA u h => by simp [ha.2.2.2 A hA u h, hb.2.2.2 A hA u h]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨ha.1.comp (c • ·), fun x y => by simp [ha.2.1], fun A u h => by simp [ha.2.2.1 A u h],
    fun A hA u h => by simp [ha.2.2.2 A hA u h]⟩

variable {R M : Type} [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]

def conjCond (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (T : M →ₗ[R] M) : Submodule R (U → M) where
  carrier := {φ | ∀ u u' : U, (∀ x, u'.1 (σ x) = σ (u.1 x)) → φ u' = T (φ u)}
  zero_mem' := by intro u u' _; simp
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb u u' h := by simp [ha u u' h, hb u u' h]
  smul_mem' c a ha u u' h := by simp [ha u u' h]

def EqHom (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) : Submodule R (U → M) :=
  XDual N U R M ⊓ ⨅ g : G F Ω, conjCond U g.toRingEquiv (ρ g)

def EqEig (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (s : R) : Submodule R (U → M) :=
  EqHom N U ρ ⊓ conjCond U σ (s • LinearMap.id)

variable (R M) in

def restr {U V : Subgroup (G F Ω)} (h : U ≤ V) : (V → M) →ₗ[R] (U → M) := LinearMap.funLeft R M (Subgroup.inclusion h)

variable (R M) in

def funRep (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [U.Normal] : Representation R (G F Ω) (U → M) where
  toFun g := LinearMap.funLeft R M (conjHom U g)
  map_one' := LinearMap.ext fun φ => funext fun u => congrArg φ (Subtype.ext (by simp [conjHom]))
  map_mul' g h := LinearMap.ext fun φ => funext fun u => congrArg φ (Subtype.ext (by simp [conjHom, mul_assoc]))

def toGal (K : Type) [Field K] [Algebra K Ω] {U : Subgroup (G F Ω)} (hU : U ≤ galOf F K Ω) : U →* G K Ω where
  toFun u := AlgEquiv.ofRingEquiv (f := u.1.toRingEquiv) (hU u.2)
  map_one' := rfl
  map_mul' _ _ := rfl

def clCond (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (T : M →ₗ[R] M) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) where
  carrier := {θ | ∀ τ : K ≃+* K, IsLift τ σ → ∀ c, θ (clMap τ c) = T (θ c)}
  zero_mem' := by intro τ _ c; simp
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb τ h c := by simp [ha τ h c, hb τ h c]
  smul_mem' r a ha τ h c := by simp [ha τ h c]

def ClEq (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (N : ℕ) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) :=
  ClDual K N R M ⊓ ⨅ g : G F Ω, clCond K g.toRingEquiv (ρ g)

def ClEig (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (N : ℕ) (ρ : Representation R (G F Ω) M) (σ : Ω ≃+* Ω) (s : R) :
    Submodule R (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) → M) :=
  ClEq K N ρ ⊓ clCond K σ (s • LinearMap.id)

def ClTw (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] (n : ℕ) (η : G F Ω →* (ZMod n)ˣ) : Subgroup (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) where
  carrier := {c | c ^ n = 1 ∧ ∀ (g : G F Ω) (τ : K ≃+* K), IsLift τ g.toRingEquiv → clMap τ c = c ^ (η g : ZMod n).val}
  one_mem' := ⟨one_pow _, fun g τ _ => by simp⟩
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨by rw [mul_pow, ha.1, hb.1, one_mul], fun g τ h => by
    rw [map_mul, ha.2 g τ h, hb.2 g τ h, mul_pow]⟩
  inv_mem' {a} ha := ⟨by rw [inv_pow, ha.1, inv_one], fun g τ h => by rw [map_inv, ha.2 g τ h, inv_pow]⟩

def ClMinus {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (τ : K ≃+* K) : Subgroup (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) :=
  (clMap τ * MonoidHom.id _).ker

def IsRad {n : ℕ} (ζ : Ω) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (φ : U → ZMod n) (β : Ω) : Prop :=
  β ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ u : U, u.1 β = ζ ^ (φ u).val * β

variable [IsAlgClosure F Ω]

instance isAlgClosure_sub (K : IntermediateField F Ω) : IsAlgClosure K Ω :=
  ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := F) K⟩

end X

section Tower

variable (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]

abbrev Uinf : Subgroup (G F Ω) := cycU l ⊤

def Fn (n : ℕ) : IntermediateField F Ω :=
  IntermediateField.adjoin F ((Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1 : Polynomial F).rootSet Ω)

instance isSplittingField_Fn (n : ℕ) : Polynomial.IsSplittingField F (Fn F Ω l n) (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1) :=
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed F
  IntermediateField.adjoin_rootSet_isSplittingField (IsAlgClosed.splits _)

instance (n : ℕ) : FiniteDimensional F (Fn F Ω l n) :=
  Polynomial.IsSplittingField.finiteDimensional _ (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1)

instance (n : ℕ) : Normal F (Fn F Ω l n) := Normal.of_isSplittingField (Polynomial.X ^ l ^ n - 1)

instance (n : ℕ) : NumberField (Fn F Ω l n) := NumberField.of_module_finite F _

abbrev Un (n : ℕ) : Subgroup (G F Ω) := levU l ⊤ n

def cycR (r : ℕ) : G F Ω →* (ZMod (l ^ r))ˣ :=
  (Units.map (PadicInt.toZModPow r : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ r)).toMonoidHom).comp (cycZ F Ω l)

def Procyc : Prop := l ≠ 2 ∨ ∀ g, cycR F Ω l 2 g = 1

variable {F Ω}

abbrev Dinf (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Submodule ℤ_[l] (cycU l U → QZ l) := XDual N (cycU l U) ℤ_[l] (QZ l)

abbrev Xinf (N : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Type := Dinf l N U →ₗ[ℤ_[l]] QZ l

end Tower

def errTwo (l : ℕ) : ℕ∞ := if l = 2 then 1 else 0

section Res

def resTriv {R Γ M U : Type} [CommRing R] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]
    [TopologicalSpace M] [IsTopologicalAddGroup M] [ContinuousConstSMul R M] (ρ : Representation R Γ M) (φ : U → Γ)
    (h : ∀ u m, ρ (φ u) m = m) : H1 ρ →ₗ[R] (U → M) :=
  Submodule.liftQ _ ((LinearMap.funLeft R M φ).comp (Z1 ρ).subtype) fun z hz => by
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 hz
    exact funext fun u => (hm (φ u)).trans (by rw [h, sub_self]; rfl)

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]

def resInf (D : LData F Ω l) (e : ℤ) : H1 (qzTw D e) →ₗ[ℤ_[l]] (Uinf F Ω l → QZ l) :=
  resTriv (qzTw D e) (fun u => toGS Ω _ u.1) fun u m => by
    have h : D.chi (toGS Ω _ u.1) = 1 := (DFunLike.congr_fun D.hchi u.1).trans u.2.2
    rw [qzTw_apply, h, one_zpow, Units.val_one, one_smul]

theorem resInf_mk (D : LData F Ω l) (e : ℤ) (z : Z1 (qzTw D e)) (u : Uinf F Ω l) :
    resInf D e (Submodule.Quotient.mk z) u = z.1 (toGS Ω _ u.1) := rfl

def zmTw (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) (e : ℤ) :
    Representation (ZMod (l ^ r)) (GS Ω (D.S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)))) (ZMod (l ^ r)) :=
  scalRep (ZMod (l ^ r))
    (((Units.map (PadicInt.toZModPow r : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod (l ^ r)).toMonoidHom).comp D.chi) ^ (-e))

def ShaZm (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) (e : ℤ) : Submodule (ZMod (l ^ r)) (H1 (zmTw D r e)) :=
  Sha1 (zmTw D r e) (locFam D.S D.A) (continuous_locFam D.S D.A)

def resZm (D : LData F Ω l) (r : ℕ) : H1 (zmTw D r 0) →ₗ[ZMod (l ^ r)] (G F Ω → ZMod (l ^ r)) :=
  resTriv (zmTw D r 0) (toGS Ω _) fun u m => by simp [zmTw]

end Res

end UpperTheory.V.Kum

end


[MFC_SRC_CL_PULL] Original class-character pullback onto XDual
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/GaloisLibrary/KummerTheory/PullOnto.lean#L1-L57
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ClPullApply
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtKer
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtFrob
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ArtSurj
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PullMem
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.ToGalDict
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PlaceDict

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum

theorem UpperTheory.V.Kum.pullOnto :
    ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra F K] [Algebra K Ω] [IsScalarTower F K Ω] [IsAlgClosure K Ω] (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) (hU : U ≤ galOf F K Ω) (N : ℕ) (R M : Type) [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M], N ≠ 0 → galOf F K Ω ≤ U → (IsTotallyComplex K ∨ ∀ m : M, 2 • m = 0 → m = 0) → (ClDual K N R M).map (clPull K hU R M) = XDual N U R M := by
  intro F Ω _ _ _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ _ _ U hU N R M _ _ _ hN hU' hK
  refine le_antisymm (pullMem F Ω K U hU N R M hN).1 fun φ hφ => ?_
  obtain ⟨hlc, hadd, hI, hD⟩ := hφ
  obtain ⟨hDec, hIne, -, -⟩ := toGalDict F Ω K U hU
  obtain ⟨hE, -, -⟩ := galEmbTop F K Ω
  let ι : G K Ω →* U := (galEmb F K Ω).codRestrict U fun σ => hU' (galEmb_mem F K Ω σ)
  have hι : Continuous ι := hE.continuous.subtype_mk _
  have h1 : ∀ σ, toGal K hU (ι σ) = σ := fun σ => AlgEquiv.ext fun x => rfl
  have h2 : ∀ u, ι (toGal K hU u) = u := fun u => Subtype.ext (AlgEquiv.ext fun x => rfl)
  let φ' : U →* Multiplicative M := MonoidHom.mk' (fun u => Multiplicative.ofAdd (φ u)) fun a b => by
    rw [hadd, ofAdd_add]
  let χ : G K Ω →* Multiplicative M := φ'.comp ι
  have hχ : ∀ σ, χ σ = 1 ↔ φ (ι σ) = 0 := fun σ => Iff.rfl
  have hker : (artCl K Ω).ker ≤ χ.ker := by
    refine artKer K Ω (Multiplicative M) χ ?_ (fun A σ hσ => ?_) fun c hc => ?_
    · exact (hlc.comp_continuous hι).isOpen_fiber 0
    · exact (hχ σ).2 (hI A _ ((hIne A _).1 ((h1 σ).symm ▸ hσ)))
    · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hc
      rcases hK with hK | hK
      · refine absurd ?_ (InfinitePlace.not_isReal_iff_isComplex.2
          (hK.isComplex (InfinitePlace.mk (j.comp (algebraMap K Ω)))))
        rw [InfinitePlace.isReal_mk_iff, ComplexEmbedding.isReal_iff]
        ext x
        have := hj (algebraMap K Ω x)
        rw [show c.toRingEquiv (algebraMap K Ω x) = algebraMap K Ω x from c.commutes x] at this
        exact this.symm
      · have hcc : c * c = 1 := AlgEquiv.ext fun x => j.injective (by
          have e1 := hj (c x)
          have e2 := hj x
          exact e1.trans ((congrArg _ e2).trans (Complex.conj_conj _)))
        have e : χ c * χ c = 1 := by rw [← map_mul, hcc, map_one]
        refine (hχ c).2 (hK _ ?_)
        rw [two_nsmul]
        exact e
  let θ := MonoidHom.liftOfSurjective (artCl K Ω) (artSurj K Ω).1 ⟨χ, hker⟩
  have hθ : ∀ σ, θ (artCl K Ω σ) = χ σ := fun σ =>
    MonoidHom.liftOfRightInverse_comp_apply _ _ _ ⟨χ, hker⟩ σ
  refine ⟨fun c => Multiplicative.toAdd (θ c), ⟨fun a b => congrArg Multiplicative.toAdd (θ.map_mul a b), fun v hv => ?_⟩, funext fun u => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_isPlaceOver Ω v
    obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := exists_isFrob hA
    show Multiplicative.toAdd (θ (primeCl v)) = 0
    rw [← artFrob K Ω A v σ hA hσ, hθ]
    exact hD A (((placeDict K Ω).2.2.2 A v N hA).2 hv) _ ((hDec A _).1 ((h1 σ).symm ▸ hσ.1))
  · rw [clPull_apply, hθ]
    exact congrArg φ (h2 u)


[MFC_SRC_CL_CARD] Original finite class-character bound
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/QuadraticExtensions/CardClDual.lean#L1-L65
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.QuadraticExtensions.ModulusAdjoinISqrtNegPrimes

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Kum UpperTheory.V.Two
open scoped Pointwise

private theorem cardLe_dual_zmod_card (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod l) V]
    [Finite V] : CardLe (V →ₗ[ZMod l] ZMod l) (Nat.card V) := by
  refine ⟨Finite.of_injective (fun f : V →ₗ[ZMod l] ZMod l => (f : V → ZMod l)) DFunLike.coe_injective, ?_⟩
  show Nat.card (Module.Dual (ZMod l) V) ≤ _
  rw [Nat.card_congr (LinearEquiv.ofFinrankEq _ _ Subspace.dual_finrank_eq).toEquiv]

private theorem cardLe_hom (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (P : Type) (θ : P → A → ZMod l)
    (hθ : Function.Injective θ) (hm : ∀ p a b, θ p (a * b) = θ p a + θ p b) :
    CardLe P (Nat.card (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range)) := by
  have hpow : ∀ p (c : A) (n : ℕ), θ p (c ^ n) = n • θ p c := by
    intro p c n
    induction n with
    | zero =>
      have h := hm p 1 1
      rw [mul_one] at h
      simpa using h
    | succ n ih => rw [pow_succ, hm, ih, succ_nsmul]
  have h1 : ∀ p, θ p 1 = 0 := fun p => by simpa using hpow p 1 0
  have hV : ∀ v : Additive (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range), l • v = 0 := by
    intro v
    change (Additive.toMul v : A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range) ^ l = 1
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective (Additive.toMul v)
    rw [← ha, ← QuotientGroup.mk_pow, QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff]
    exact ⟨a, rfl⟩
  let φ : P → A →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := fun p =>
    { toFun := fun a => Multiplicative.ofAdd (θ p a)
      map_one' := by
        show Multiplicative.ofAdd (θ p 1) = 1
        rw [h1]
        rfl
      map_mul' := fun a b => by
        show Multiplicative.ofAdd (θ p (a * b)) = _
        rw [hm]
        rfl }
  let ψ : P → (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range) →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := fun p =>
    QuotientGroup.lift _ (φ p) (by
      rintro _ ⟨c, rfl⟩
      show Multiplicative.ofAdd (θ p (c ^ l)) = 1
      rw [hpow, nsmul_eq_mul, ZMod.natCast_self, zero_mul]
      rfl)
  let Φ : P → (Additive (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range) →+ ZMod l) := fun p =>
    AddMonoidHom.mk' (fun v => Multiplicative.toAdd (ψ p (Additive.toMul v))) (fun u v => by
      show Multiplicative.toAdd (ψ p (Additive.toMul u * Additive.toMul v)) = _
      rw [map_mul]
      rfl)
  have hΦ : ∀ p (a : A), Φ p (Additive.ofMul (QuotientGroup.mk a)) = θ p a := fun p a => rfl
  have hinj : Function.Injective Φ := by
    intro p q h
    apply hθ
    funext a
    rw [← hΦ p a, ← hΦ q a, h]
  letI : Module (ZMod l) (Additive (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range)) := AddCommGroup.zmodModule hV
  exact CardLe.of_injective (cardLe_dual_zmod_card l (Additive (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range))) _
    ((AddMonoidHom.toZModLinearMap_injective l (M := Additive (A ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : A →* A).range))
      (M₁ := ZMod l)).comp hinj)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Two.cardClDual :
    ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (N : ℕ), CardLe (ClDual K N (ZMod l) (ZMod l)) (Nat.card (ClassGroup (𝓞 K) ⧸ (powMonoidHom l : ClassGroup (𝓞 K) →* ClassGroup (𝓞 K)).range)) := by
  intro K _ _ l _ N
  exact cardLe_hom (ClassGroup (𝓞 K)) l _ (fun θ => θ.1) Subtype.val_injective fun θ => θ.2.1


[MFC_SRC_FIELD] Literal field_ex; inherited CAF8
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/FieldEx.lean#L1-L155
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem fix_fix {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (H : Subgroup (Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω))
    (h : IsOpen (H : Set (Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω))) : (IntermediateField.fixedField H).fixingSubgroup = H :=
  InfiniteGalois.fixingSubgroup_fixedField ⟨H, H.isClosed_of_isOpen h⟩

private theorem commutator_le_ker_of_hom_int_units {Γ : Type*} [Group Γ] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) : commutator Γ ≤ η.ker := by
  rw [commutator_def, Subgroup.commutator_le]
  intro a _ b _
  rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_commutatorElement, commutatorElement_eq_one_iff_mul_comm]
  exact mul_comm _ _

private theorem conj_ne_self_of_sq_eq_neg_natCast (z : ℂ) (D : ℕ) (hD : 0 < D) (h : z ^ 2 = -(D : ℂ)) :
    starRingEnd ℂ z ≠ z := by
  intro hc
  have him : z.im = 0 := Complex.conj_eq_iff_im.mp hc
  have h1 := congrArg Complex.re h
  simp [pow_two, him] at h1
  have hD' : (0 : ℝ) < D := by exact_mod_cast hD
  nlinarith [mul_self_nonneg z.re]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.field_ex :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (m : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), UFacts l η Ψ M m U → ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω), IsAbelianGalois ℚ L ∧ upl Ω L = U ∧ galOf ℚ L Ω = U ⊓ η.ker := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m U hU
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ2, hδ⟩ := hG.quad
  have hδ0 : δ ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    simp at hδ2
    omega
  have hker : ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, η s = 1 ↔ s δ = δ := by
    intro s
    constructor
    · intro h
      rw [hδ s, h]
      simp
    · intro h
      rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η s) with h1 | h1
      · exact h1
      · exfalso
        rw [hδ s, h1] at h
        simp at h
        have h2 : (2 : Ω) * δ = 0 := by linear_combination -h
        rcases mul_eq_zero.mp h2 with h3 | h3
        · exact two_ne_zero h3
        · exact hδ0 h3
  have hint : IsIntegral ℚ δ := Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral δ
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ (IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {δ}) :=
    IntermediateField.adjoin.finiteDimensional hint
  have hkerO : IsOpen (η.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
    refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono ?_ (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen (IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {δ}))
    intro s hs
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hker]
    exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff _ _).mp hs δ
      (IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ δ)
  have hHo : IsOpen ((U ⊓ η.ker : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
    rw [Subgroup.coe_inf]
    exact hU.isOpen.inter hkerO
  have hcomm : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ U ⊓ η.ker := le_inf hU.ab (commutator_le_ker_of_hom_int_units η)
  set L := IntermediateField.fixedField (U ⊓ η.ker) with hLdef
  have hfix : L.fixingSubgroup = U ⊓ η.ker := fix_fix _ hHo
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ L := (InfiniteGalois.isOpen_iff_finite L).mp (by rw [hfix]; exact hHo)
  haveI : NumberField L := ⟨⟩
  have hN : (L.fixingSubgroup).Normal := by
    rw [hfix]
    refine ⟨fun n hn g => ?_⟩
    have hc : g * n * g⁻¹ * n⁻¹ ∈ U ⊓ η.ker :=
      hcomm (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top g) (Subgroup.mem_top n))
    have he : g * n * g⁻¹ = g * n * g⁻¹ * n⁻¹ * n := by
      group
    rw [he]
    exact Subgroup.mul_mem _ hc hn
  have hAb : @IsAbelianGalois ℚ L _ _ (IntermediateField.algebra' L) := by
    letI : Algebra ℚ L := IntermediateField.algebra' L
    haveI hg : IsGalois ℚ L := (InfiniteGalois.normal_iff_isGalois L).mp hN
    haveI : IsMulCommutative Gal(L/ℚ) := ⟨⟨fun a b => by
      obtain ⟨a', rfl⟩ := AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom_surjective Ω a
      obtain ⟨b', rfl⟩ := AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom_surjective Ω b
      have hc : a' * b' * a'⁻¹ * b'⁻¹ ∈ (AlgEquiv.restrictNormalHom (F := ℚ) (K₁ := Ω) L).ker := by
        rw [IntermediateField.restrictNormalHom_ker, hfix]
        exact hcomm (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top a') (Subgroup.mem_top b'))
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_inv] at hc
      exact mul_inv_eq_iff_eq_mul.mp (mul_inv_eq_one.mp hc)⟩⟩
    exact ⟨⟩
  haveI : IsAbelianGalois ℚ L := by
    convert hAb <;> exact Subsingleton.elim _ _
  have hδL : δ ∈ L := by
    rw [IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff]
    intro s hs
    exact (hker s).mp (MonoidHom.mem_ker.mp (Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hs).2)
  have hδL2 : (⟨δ, hδL⟩ : L) ^ 2 = -(D : L) := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    simpa using hδ2
  haveI : NumberField.IsTotallyComplex L := ⟨fun v => by
    rw [← NumberField.InfinitePlace.not_isReal_iff_isComplex, NumberField.InfinitePlace.isReal_iff]
    intro hv
    apply conj_ne_self_of_sq_eq_neg_natCast (v.embedding ⟨δ, hδL⟩) D hD
    · rw [← map_pow, hδL2]
      simp
    · have := RingHom.congr_fun hv ⟨δ, hδL⟩
      rwa [NumberField.ComplexEmbedding.conjugate_coe_eq] at this⟩
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.cc
  have hσU : σ ∈ U := hU.real σ hG.cc
  have hησ : η σ = -1 := by
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η σ) with h | h
    · exfalso
      have hσδ : σ δ = δ := (hker σ).mp h
      apply conj_ne_self_of_sq_eq_neg_natCast (j δ) D hD
      · rw [← map_pow, hδ2]
        simp
      · rw [← hj, hσδ]
    · exact h
  have hcc : ∀ z : L, σ (z : Ω) = ((NumberField.IsCMField.complexConj L z : L) : Ω) := by
    intro z
    apply j.injective
    rw [hj]
    exact (NumberField.IsCMField.complexEmbedding_complexConj L (j.comp (algebraMap L Ω)) z).symm
  refine ⟨L, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, ?_, ?_⟩
  · ext s
    change (∀ x ∈ NumberField.maximalRealSubfield L, s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x) ↔ s ∈ U
    constructor
    · intro h
      rw [← fix_fix U hU.isOpen, IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff]
      intro y hy
      have hyL : y ∈ L := (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).mpr fun f hf =>
        (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).mp hy f (Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hf).1
      have hre : (⟨y, hyL⟩ : L) ∈ NumberField.maximalRealSubfield L := by
        rw [← NumberField.IsCMField.complexConj_eq_self_iff]
        apply Subtype.ext
        rw [← hcc]
        exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).mp hy σ hσU
      exact h _ hre
    · intro hs x hx
      have hσx : σ (x : Ω) = x := by
        rw [hcc, (NumberField.IsCMField.complexConj_eq_self_iff L x).mpr hx]
      show s (x : Ω) = x
      rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η s) with h1 | h1
      · exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).mp x.2 s (Subgroup.mem_inf.mpr ⟨hs, h1⟩)
      · have hm : σ * s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker := by
          refine Subgroup.mem_inf.mpr ⟨U.mul_mem hσU hs, ?_⟩
          rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, hησ, h1]
          rfl
        have := (IntermediateField.mem_fixedField_iff _ _).mp x.2 (σ * s) hm
        rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply] at this
        apply σ.injective
        rw [this, hσx]
  · ext s
    change (∀ x : L, s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x) ↔ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker
    rw [← hfix, IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff]
    constructor
    · intro h x hx
      exact h ⟨x, hx⟩
    · intro h x
      exact h x x.2


[MFC_SRC_AUXW] Original auxw_ex; inherited CAF5

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem ax_cycMod_one {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime]
    {s : G ℚ Ω} (hs : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ l = 1 → s ζ = ζ) : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s = 1 := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  haveI : ∀ i, HasEnoughRootsOfUnity Ω (l ^ i) := fun i => inferInstance
  apply Units.ext
  show PadicInt.toZModPow 1 ((cyclotomicCharacter Ω l (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G ℚ Ω) Ω s)).val) = 1
  rw [cyclotomicCharacter.toZModPow]
  symm
  apply modularCyclotomicCharacter.unique
  intro t ht
  have ht' : (t : Ω) ^ l = 1 := by
    have := congrArg Units.val ((mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).1 ht)
    simpa using this
  have hl1 : l ^ 1 ≠ 1 := by
    have := hl.out.one_lt
    rw [pow_one]
    omega
  rw [ZMod.val_one'' hl1, pow_one]
  exact hs _ ht'

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.auxw_ex :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 → ∃ w, FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w ∧ w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨a, hη, hbar⟩ := hG.cyc
  have hM := hG.M_ne
  have hlM := hG.l_dvd
  have hL : M ^ (a + 1) * Q ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hM) hQ
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hwgt, hw1⟩ := Nat.exists_prime_gt_modEq_one (M ^ (a + 1) * Q) hL
  have hMaL : M ^ a ∣ M ^ (a + 1) * Q := Dvd.dvd.mul_right (pow_dvd_pow M (Nat.le_succ a)) Q
  have hlL : l ∣ M ^ (a + 1) * Q :=
    dvd_trans hlM (dvd_trans (dvd_pow_self M (Nat.succ_ne_zero a)) (dvd_mul_right _ _))
  have hML : M ∣ M ^ (a + 1) * Q := dvd_trans (dvd_pow_self M (Nat.succ_ne_zero a)) (dvd_mul_right _ _)
  have hndvd : ∀ m, m ∣ M ^ (a + 1) * Q → ¬ w ∣ m := by
    intro m hm hwm
    have h1 := Nat.le_of_dvd (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hL) hm
    have h2 := Nat.le_of_dvd (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (ne_zero_of_dvd_ne_zero hL hm)) hwm
    omega
  refine ⟨w, ⟨hw, hndvd M hML, Nat.ModEq.of_dvd hlL hw1, ?_⟩, Nat.ModEq.of_dvd (dvd_mul_left Q _) hw1⟩
  intro B' hB' s hs
  haveI := Fact.mk hw
  have fix : ∀ m, m ∣ M ^ (a + 1) * Q → ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ m = 1 → s ζ = ζ := by
    intro m hm
    have hcop : m.Coprime w := ((Nat.Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd hw).2 (hndvd m hm)).symm
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank.decActRoots ℚ Ω w B' hB' m hcop s hs
    intro ζ hζ
    rw [hk ζ hζ]
    have hwk : w ^ k ≡ 1 [MOD m] := by
      have := (Nat.ModEq.of_dvd hm hw1).pow k
      rwa [one_pow] at this
    rw [pow_eq_pow_mod _ hζ, show w ^ k % m = 1 % m from hwk, ← pow_eq_pow_mod 1 hζ, pow_one]
  refine Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · exact hη s (fix _ hMaL)
  · exact hbar s (fix _ hMaL)
  · rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    change (Units.map _) ((cycMod ℚ Ω l 1) s) = 1
    rw [ax_cycMod_one l (fix l hlL), map_one]


[MFC_SRC_SEED] Original seed_cl; inherited CAF7

private theorem km_mono {Ω C : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [Monoid C] {m n : ℕ} (h : m ∣ n) {χ : G ℚ Ω →* C}
    (hχ : Af.KillsMu m χ) : Af.KillsMu n χ := fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => hs ζ (by
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := h
  rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow])

private theorem split_of {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {N w : ℕ} (hw : w ≡ 1 [MOD N])
    {χ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (hχ : Af.KillsMu N χ) : Sd.SplitAt N w χ :=
  ⟨hχ, fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => by
    rw [hs ζ hζ, pow_eq_pow_mod w hζ, hw, ← pow_eq_pow_mod 1 hζ, pow_one]⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.seed_cl :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∃ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (w : ℕ), IsAbelianGalois ℚ L → galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] → upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w → (∃ s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω, barChar c.O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1) → SeedClause c η Ψ σ L := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ c _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨a, hηa, hΨa⟩ := hG.cyc
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.Ψ_l
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hηδ⟩ := hG.quad
  obtain ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, hK2, hKc, hKη⟩ := sc_quad Ω η D δ hD hδ hηδ
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hTa⟩ := sc_teich Ω l c hG.fin
  have hM : M ^ a * l ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hG.M_ne) hlp.ne_zero
  obtain ⟨r, N, hlN, hN⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_pow_mul_and_not_dvd hM l hlp.one_lt.ne'
  have hN0 : N ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    exact hM (by rw [hN, mul_zero])
  let φ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField c.O)ˣ := barChar c.O Ψ * (omO Ω l c.O ^ j)⁻¹
  let χ' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := T.comp φ
  let η' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom).comp η
  have hφ : ∀ s, φ s = barChar c.O Ψ s * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹ := fun s => rfl
  have hχ : ∀ s, χ' s = T (φ s) := fun s => rfl
  have hη'ker : η'.ker = η.ker := by
    ext s
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    exact map_eq_one_iff _ (Units.map_injective (f := (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom) Int.cast_injective)
  have hφK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) φ := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : barChar c.O Ψ s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hΨa s hs
    have h2 : omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_left _ _) (sc_om Ω l c.O).1 s hs
    rw [hφ, h1, h2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one]
  have hχK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) χ' := by
    intro s hs
    rw [hχ, hφK s hs, map_one]
  have hη'K : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) η' := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : η s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hηa s hs
    show Units.map _ (η s) = 1
    rw [h1, map_one]
  have hχN : Af.KillsMu N χ' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ χ' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hχK) B hG.over_l ?_
    intro s hs
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hχ, hφ, hj B hG.over_l s hs, mul_inv_cancel, map_one]
  have hη'N : Af.KillsMu N η' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ η' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hη'K) B hG.over_l ?_
    rw [hη'ker]
    exact hG.η_l B hG.over_l
  have hT1 : T (-1) = -1 := by
    have hne : (-1 : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) ≠ 1 := by
      intro h
      have h' : (-1 : ResidueField c.O) = 1 := by
        simpa using congrArg (fun u : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ => (u : ResidueField c.O)) h
      rw [neg_one_eq_one_iff, ringChar.eq (ResidueField c.O) l] at h'
      exact hG.odd h'
    have hsq : ((T (-1) : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l) * (T (-1) : Qbar l) = 1 := by
      rw [← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, neg_one_mul, neg_neg, map_one, Units.val_one]
    rcases mul_self_eq_one_iff.1 hsq with h | h
    · exact absurd (hT (by rw [map_one]; exact Units.ext h)) hne
    · exact Units.ext (by rw [h, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one])
  have happ : ∀ s, (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s =
      (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l) s ^ j * T ((Units.map ↑(residue c.O)) (Ψ s) * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹) :=
    fun s => rfl
  have hord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' := by
    classical
    haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField c.O)
    have hpow : χ' ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1 := MonoidHom.ext fun s => by
      show (T (φ s)) ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1
      rw [← map_pow, pow_card_eq_one, map_one]
    intro hd
    have hd2 := hd.trans (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hpow)
    obtain ⟨n, -, hcard⟩ := FiniteField.card (ResidueField c.O) l
    rw [Fintype.card_units, hcard] at hd2
    have hl1 : l ∣ l ^ (n : ℕ) := dvd_pow_self l n.ne_zero
    have hpos : 1 ≤ l ^ (n : ℕ) := Nat.one_le_pow _ _ hlp.pos
    have h3 : l ∣ (l ^ (n : ℕ) - 1) + 1 := by
      rw [Nat.sub_add_cancel hpos]
      exact hl1
    exact hlp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.1 ((Nat.dvd_add_right hd2).1 h3))
  refine ⟨N, hN0, ?_⟩
  intro L _ _ _ _ w hLab hLgal hw hwQ hLth hs0
  obtain ⟨s0, hs0L, hs0⟩ := hs0
  obtain ⟨θ, hθker, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩ := sc_theta Ω l w hw.prime hw.one
  have hwN : ¬ w ∣ N := by
    intro hd
    have hd' : w ∣ M ^ a * l := hN ▸ dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hd _
    rcases (Nat.Prime.dvd_mul hw.prime).1 hd' with h | h
    · exact hw.cop (hw.prime.dvd_of_dvd_pow h)
    · have hwl := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hw.prime hlp).1 h
      have h1 : w % l = 1 % l := hw.one
      rw [hwl, Nat.mod_self, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt hlp.one_lt] at h1
      exact zero_ne_one h1
  have hLK : galOf ℚ L Ω ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    rw [hKη, hLgal]
    exact inf_le_right
  refine ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, N, w, χ', η', θ, j, ?_, ?_, hord, fun s => rfl, fun s => happ s ▸ hTa (Ψ s) j s, ?_⟩
  · refine ⟨hG.cc, ⟨hG.odd, Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN0, hlN, hw.prime, hwN, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩, hK2, hKc, hLab, hLK,
      by rw [hη'ker, hKη], split_of hwQ hη'N, split_of hwQ hχN, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [hθker, hLgal]
      exact inf_le_left.trans hLth
    · rw [hχ, hφ, hG.even, (sc_om Ω l c.O).2 σ hG.cc, one_mul, ← inv_pow, inv_neg_one, map_pow, hT1]
    · refine ⟨s0, hs0L, fun hc => hs0 ?_⟩
      exact sc_cong l c (Ψ s0) _ (happ s0 ▸ hTa (Ψ s0) j s0) hc
  · intro heq
    apply hG.g4
    intro s hs
    rw [Subgroup.mem_inf, MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hs
    have h1 : χ' s = 1 := by
      rw [hχ, hφ, hs.1, hs.2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one, map_one]
    rw [heq] at h1
    rw [← hη'ker]
    exact h1
  · exact sc_disc Ω l K hK2 (fun B' hB' => hKη ▸ hG.η_l B' hB')

private theorem tf (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] : Module.IsTorsionFree F Ω :=
  DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

private theorem deg_eq (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)} (hA : IsPlaceOver A w)
    (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal) : locDeg w = (galOf ℚ F Ω).relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) A) := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := @Loc.decEmb F Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ l _
  obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ := h1 A w hA hw
  obtain ⟨-, φ, -, -, hr, -, -, hd⟩ := h2 j
  obtain ⟨-, φ₀, -, h0, hr0, -⟩ := (@Loc.decEmb ℚ Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ l _).2 j
  have hs : Function.Surjective φ₀ := fun s => (hr0 s).2 fun x => by simp
  rw [← hd w hA, ← Subgroup.index_comap_of_surjective _ hs, Subgroup.relIndex]
  congr 1
  ext g
  rw [Subgroup.mem_comap, hr, Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf, mem_galOf]
  exact forall_congr' fun x => by rw [h0]; exact j.injective.eq_iff

omit [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] in
private theorem cc_ex [IsTotallyReal F] (w : InfinitePlace F) : ∃ c : G F Ω, IsCCAt w c := by
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic F Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
  haveI : IsGalois F Ω := {}
  letI : Algebra F ℂ := w.embedding.toAlgebra
  let j : Ω →ₐ[F] ℂ := IsAlgClosed.lift
  have hr : ComplexEmbedding.IsReal w.embedding := InfinitePlace.isReal_iff.1 (IsTotallyReal.isReal w)
  let cj : ℂ →ₐ[F] ℂ := { starRingEnd ℂ with commutes' := fun x => RingHom.congr_fun hr x }
  letI : Algebra Ω ℂ := j.toRingHom.toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower F Ω ℂ := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun x => (j.commutes x).symm
  refine ⟨(cj.comp j).restrictNormal' Ω, j.toRingHom, fun x => ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact AlgHom.restrictNormal_commutes (cj.comp j) Ω x
  · rw [show j.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap F Ω) = w.embedding from RingHom.ext fun x => j.commutes x]
    exact w.mk_embedding

private theorem kills_open {N : ℕ} (hN : N ≠ 0) {C : Type} [Group C] {χ : G ℚ Ω →* C} (h : Af.KillsMu N χ) :
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
  have hf : {ζ : Ω | ζ ^ N = 1}.Finite :=
    (Polynomial.nthRootsFinset N (1 : Ω)).finite_toSet.subset fun ζ hζ =>
      (Polynomial.mem_nthRootsFinset (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN) _).2 hζ
  haveI := hf.to_subtype
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ (IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {ζ : Ω | ζ ^ N = 1}) :=
    IntermediateField.finiteDimensional_adjoin fun x _ => Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
  refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := (IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {ζ : Ω | ζ ^ N = 1}).fixingSubgroup) (fun s hs => h s fun ζ hζ => ?_)
    (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen _)
  exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff _ _).1 hs ζ (IntermediateField.subset_adjoin _ _ hζ)

omit [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] in
private theorem eta_cc {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {σ : G ℚ Ω} (hσ : IsCC σ) {D : ℕ} {δ : Ω} (hD : 0 < D) (h2 : δ ^ 2 = -(D : Ω))
    (h : ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (η s : ℤ) • δ) : η σ = -1 := by
  refine (Int.units_eq_one_or (η σ)).resolve_left fun h1 => ?_
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hσ
  have h3 : σ δ = δ := by rw [h, h1]; simp
  have h4 := hj δ
  rw [h3] at h4
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := Complex.conj_eq_iff_real.1 h4.symm
  have h5 := congrArg j h2
  rw [map_pow, hr, map_neg, map_natCast] at h5
  have h6 : r ^ 2 = -(D : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h5
  have h7 : (0 : ℝ) < D := by exact_mod_cast hD
  nlinarith [sq_nonneg r]

omit [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] in
private theorem over_smul {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)} (hA : IsPlaceOver A w) (g : G F Ω) :
    IsPlaceOver (g • A) w := fun x => by
  have e : ∀ y : F, algebraMap F Ω y ∈ g • A ↔ algebraMap F Ω y ∈ A := fun y => by
    conv_lhs => rw [← g.commutes y]
    exact ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul_iff
  rw [← hA x, valuation_lt_one_iff', valuation_lt_one_iff', ← map_inv₀, e, e]

omit [IsAlgClosure F Ω] in
private theorem emb_smul (g : G F Ω) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) : galEmb ℚ F Ω g • A = g • A := rfl

private theorem conj_l (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {A B : ValuationSubring Ω} (hA : A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1)
    (hB : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) : ∃ g : G ℚ Ω, g • A = B := by
  obtain ⟨a1, a2, a3, -⟩ := places ℚ Ω l A
  obtain ⟨b1, b2, b3, -⟩ := places ℚ Ω l B
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := a1 fun h => (a2 h).2 hA
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := b1 fun h => (b2 h).2 hB
  have key : ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal → v.asIdeal = Ideal.span {((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)} := fun v h => by
    have hp : Prime ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) := by
      rw [← MulEquiv.prime_iff Rat.ringOfIntegersEquiv, map_natCast]
      exact Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.1 Fact.out
    exact (((Ideal.span_singleton_prime hp.ne_zero).2 hp).isMaximal (mt Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot.1 hp.ne_zero)).eq_of_le v.isPrime.ne_top
      ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).2 h) |>.symm
  have e : v = u := HeightOneSpectrum.ext ((key v ((a3 v hv).1 hA)).trans (key u ((b3 u hu).1 hB)).symm)
  exact exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver hv (e ▸ hu)

private theorem place_ex (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {c : G F Ω} (hc : η (galEmb ℚ F Ω c) = -1)
    {B : ValuationSubring Ω} (hB : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) (hv : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal) :
    ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v ∧ ∃ γ : G ℚ Ω, η γ = 1 ∧ A = γ • B := by
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := exists_isPlaceOver Ω v
  obtain ⟨γ, rfl⟩ := conj_l l hB (((places F Ω l A).2.2.1 v hA).2 hv)
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η γ) with h | h
  · exact ⟨_, hA, γ, h, rfl⟩
  · exact ⟨_, over_smul hA c, galEmb ℚ F Ω c * γ, by rw [map_mul, hc, h]; simp, by rw [mul_smul, emb_smul]⟩

omit [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] in
private theorem tame_le (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K H : Subgroup (G F Ω)) [H.Normal] (hH : IsClosed (H : Set (G F Ω)))
    (h : ∀ v ∉ S, ∀ A, IsPlaceOver A v → Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K ≤ H) : GC.tameN S K ≤ H := by
  refine Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal fun s hs => ?_) hH
  simp only [Set.mem_iUnion] at hs
  obtain ⟨v, hv, A, hA, hs⟩ := hs
  exact h v hv A hA hs

private theorem clos_comap {X Y : Type} [Group X] [Group Y] [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [IsTopologicalGroup X]
    [IsTopologicalGroup Y] (e : X ≃ₜ* Y) (s : Set X) (t : Set Y) (h1 : ∀ x ∈ s, x = 1 ∨ e x ∈ t) (h2 : ∀ x, e x ∈ t → x ∈ s) :
    (Subgroup.normalClosure t).topologicalClosure.comap (e : X →* Y) = (Subgroup.normalClosure s).topologicalClosure := by
  have h : (Subgroup.normalClosure t).comap (e : X →* Y) = Subgroup.normalClosure s := by
    refine le_antisymm ?_ (Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal fun x hx => ?_)
    · rw [show (e : X →* Y) = (e.toMulEquiv : X →* Y) from rfl, ← Subgroup.comap_normalClosure]
      exact Subgroup.normalClosure_mono h2
    · rcases h1 x hx with rfl | h
      · exact one_mem _
      · exact Subgroup.subset_normalClosure h
  ext x
  rw [← h]
  exact Set.ext_iff.1 (e.toHomeomorph.preimage_closure (Subgroup.normalClosure t : Set Y)) x

private theorem upl_eq (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] :
    upl Ω L = galOf ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω := by
  ext s
  exact ⟨fun h x => h x x.2, fun h x hx => h ⟨x, hx⟩⟩

private theorem equiv_ex (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure (W.Lp L) Ω] : ∃ e : G (W.Lp L) Ω ≃ₜ* upl Ω L, ∀ g, (e g).1 = galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g := by
  have hE := (galEmbTop ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω).1
  have hr : (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω).range = upl Ω L := (range_galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω).trans (upl_eq Ω L).symm
  let e : G (W.Lp L) Ω ≃* upl Ω L := (MonoidHom.ofInjective hE.injective).trans (MulEquiv.subgroupCongr hr)
  refine ⟨{ e with continuous_toFun := hE.continuous.subtype_mk _, continuous_invFun := hE.continuous_iff.2 ?_ }, fun _ => rfl⟩
  convert continuous_subtype_val using 1
  funext s
  exact congrArg Subtype.val (e.apply_symm_apply s)

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.link_ex :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (m : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L) → IsAbelianGalois ℚ L → galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker → g₁ ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω → ∃ Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O), Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb g m L _ _ _ _ g₁ U hab hgal hg₁
  classical
  haveI := tf L Ω
  haveI := tf (W.Lp L) Ω
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ,
    haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic; Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) L⟩
  haveI : IsGalois (W.Lp L) Ω := {}
  haveI := g.fin
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := equiv_ex Ω L
  have he' : ∀ s : upl Ω L, galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω (e.symm s) = s := fun s => by rw [← he, e.apply_symm_apply]
  obtain ⟨hDI, -, hcyc, -⟩ := galEmbFam ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω l
  have hcont := (galEmbTop ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω).1.continuous
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hηδ⟩ := g.quad
  have hσ : η σ = -1 := eta_cc g.cc hD hδ hηδ
  have hσU : σ ∈ upl Ω L := U.real σ g.cc
  have hg : g₁ ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker := hgal ▸ hg₁
  obtain ⟨a, hk1, hk2⟩ := g.cyc
  have ho1 := kills_open (pow_ne_zero a g.M_ne) hk1
  have ho2 := kills_open (pow_ne_zero a g.M_ne) hk2
  set E := galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω with hE
  set K : Subgroup (G (W.Lp L) Ω) := (η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap E with hK
  have hKo : IsOpen (K : Set (G (W.Lp L) Ω)) := (ho1.inter ho2).preimage hcont
  have hKi : ¬ l ∣ K.index := by
    have h1 : K = ((η.prod (barChar O Ψ)).comp E).ker := by rw [hK, ← MonoidHom.comap_ker, MonoidHom.ker_prod]
    rw [h1, Subgroup.index_ker]
    intro h
    have h2 := h.trans (Subgroup.card_subgroup_dvd_card _)
    rw [Nat.card_prod, hl.out.dvd_mul] at h2
    rcases h2 with h2 | h2
    · rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_units_int] at h2
      exact g.odd ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).1 h2)
    · haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField O)
      obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_prime_orderOf_dvd_card' l h2
      have h3 : ((x : ResidueField O) - 1) ^ l = 0 := by
        rw [sub_pow_char, one_pow, ← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ← hx, pow_orderOf_eq_one, Units.val_one, sub_self]
      have h4 : x = 1 := Units.ext (sub_eq_zero.1 (pow_eq_zero_iff hl.out.ne_zero |>.1 h3))
      rw [h4, orderOf_one] at hx
      exact hl.out.one_lt.ne hx
  have hfin : {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 (W.Lp L)) | ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (W.Lp L)) ∈ v.asIdeal}.Finite := by
    refine (Ideal.finite_factors (I := Ideal.span {((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (W.Lp L))})
      (mt Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot.1 (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 hl.out.ne_zero))).subset fun v hv => ?_
    exact Ideal.dvd_span_singleton.2 hv
  let S := hfin.toFinset
  have hS : ∀ v, v ∈ S ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (W.Lp L)) ∈ v.asIdeal := fun v => hfin.mem_toFinset
  have hval : ∀ {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 (W.Lp L))}, IsPlaceOver A v →
      (A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (W.Lp L)) ∈ v.asIdeal) := fun {A v} h => (places (W.Lp L) Ω l A).2.2.1 v h
  set N := GC.tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 (W.Lp L)))) K with hN
  haveI : N.Normal := Subgroup.is_normal_topologicalClosure _
  haveI hNc : IsClosed (N : Set (G (W.Lp L) Ω)) := Subgroup.isClosed_topologicalClosure _
  have hNK : N ≤ K := tame_le _ _ _ (K.isClosed_of_isOpen hKo) fun _ _ _ _ => inf_le_right
  have hNc' : N ≤ (cycZ (W.Lp L) Ω l).ker :=
    tame_le _ _ _ (isClosed_singleton.preimage (continuous_cycZ _ _ l)) fun v hv A hA =>
      inf_le_left.trans ((places (W.Lp L) Ω l A).2.2.2 fun h => hv ((hS v).2 ((hval hA).1 h)))
  have hc : η (E (e.symm ⟨σ, hσU⟩)) = -1 := by rw [he']; exact hσ
  choose A hA hAγ using fun v : W.Vl l L => place_ex l hc g.over_l v.1 v.2
  have hAl : ∀ v, (A v).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun v => (hval (hA v)).2 v.2
  have hAM : ∀ v, (A v).valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := fun v => by
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := g.l_dvd
    rw [hk, Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    exact mul_lt_one_of_lt_of_le (hAl v) (valuation_natCast_le_one _ k)
  choose c hcc using fun w : InfinitePlace (W.Lp L) => cc_ex (Ω := Ω) w
  have hDec : ∀ (B' : ValuationSubring Ω) s, s ∈ Dec (F := W.Lp L) B' ↔ E s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B' := fun B' s => by
    rw [← (hDI B').1]; rfl
  have hIne : ∀ (B' : ValuationSubring Ω) s, s ∈ Ine (F := W.Lp L) B' ↔ E s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' := fun B' s => by
    rw [← (hDI B').2]; rfl
  have hEU : ∀ s, E s ∈ upl Ω L := fun s => he s ▸ (e s).2
  let π : G (W.Lp L) Ω →* G (W.Lp L) Ω ⧸ N := QuotientGroup.mk' N
  let del : G (W.Lp L) Ω ⧸ N →* ℤˣ := QuotientGroup.lift N (η.comp E) fun s hs => (hNK hs).1
  let Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O) :=
    { Γ := G (W.Lp L) Ω ⧸ N
      td := (AlgebraTools.totallyDisconnected_quot _ N hNc).1
      del := del
      c := π (e.symm ⟨σ, hσU⟩)
      V := W.Vl l L
      fV := haveI : Finite (W.Vl l L) := hfin.to_subtype; Fintype.ofFinite _
      Gv := fun v => Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)
      cv := fun v => isCompact_iff_compactSpace.1 (isClosed_Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).isCompact
      loc0 := fun v => (π.comp (Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).subtype).codRestrict _ fun s =>
        U.split (A v) (hAM v) ⟨(hDec _ _).1 s.2, hEU _⟩
      Iv := fun v => (Ine (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).comap (Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).subtype
      dv := fun v => locDeg v.1
      Inf := InfinitePlace (W.Lp L)
      cw := fun w => π (c w)
      eps := QuotientGroup.lift N (cycZ (W.Lp L) Ω l) hNc'
      Ψ := QuotientGroup.lift N ((barChar O Ψ).comp E) fun s hs => (hNK hs).2
      g0 := ⟨π (e.symm ⟨g₁, hg.1⟩), (congrArg η (he' ⟨g₁, hg.1⟩)).trans hg.2⟩ }
  let k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ :=
    { ab := hab
      gal := hgal
      unr := U.unr
      split := U.split
      teich := U.teich
      p := π.comp (e.symm : upl Ω L →* G (W.Lp L) Ω)
      p_cont := QuotientGroup.continuous_mk.comp e.symm.continuous
      p_surj := (QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective N).comp e.symm.surjective
      p_ker := by
        rw [← MonoidHom.comap_ker, QuotientGroup.ker_mk']
        refine clos_comap e.symm _ _ ?_ fun x hx => ?_
        · rintro x ⟨B', hB', h1, h2, h3⟩
          obtain ⟨a1, a2, -⟩ := places (W.Lp L) Ω l B'
          have hx : e.symm x ∈ Ine (F := W.Lp L) B' := by rw [hIne, he']; exact h1
          by_cases ht : B' = ⊤
          · rw [(a2 ht).1, Subgroup.mem_bot] at hx
            exact Or.inl (e.symm.injective (hx.trans (map_one _).symm))
          · obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := a1 ht
            refine Or.inr ?_
            simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
            refine ⟨v, fun h => hB' ((hval hv).2 ((hS v).1 h)), B', hv, hx, ?_⟩
            show E (e.symm x) ∈ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker
            rw [he']
            exact ⟨h2, h3⟩
        · simp only [Set.mem_iUnion] at hx
          obtain ⟨v, hv, B', hB', h1, h2⟩ := hx
          have h3 : E (e.symm x) ∈ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker := h2
          have h4 := (hIne _ _).1 h1
          rw [he'] at h3 h4
          exact ⟨B', fun h => hv ((hS v).2 ((hval hB').1 h)), h4, h3.1, h3.2⟩
      del := fun s => congrArg η (he' s)
      eps := fun s => (DFunLike.congr_fun hcyc (e.symm s)).symm.trans (congrArg (cycZ ℚ Ω l) (he' s))
      psi := fun s => congrArg (barChar O Ψ) (he' s)
      c := ⟨hσU, rfl⟩
      g0 := ⟨hg.1, rfl⟩
      pl := A
      pl_conj := hAγ
      pl_all := fun B' hB' => by
        obtain ⟨a1, a2, -⟩ := places (W.Lp L) Ω l B'
        obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := a1 fun h => (a2 h).2 hB'
        obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (hA ⟨v, (hval hv).1 hB'⟩) hv
        refine ⟨⟨v, (hval hv).1 hB'⟩, ⟨E u, hEU u, hu.symm⟩, ?_⟩
        rintro v' ⟨u', hu', h⟩
        have h1 := over_smul (hA v') (e.symm ⟨u', hu'⟩)
        rw [← emb_smul, he', ← h] at h1
        exact Subtype.ext (eq_of_isPlaceOver h1 hv)
      j := fun v => (e : G (W.Lp L) Ω →* upl Ω L).comp (Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).subtype
      j_cont := fun v => e.continuous.comp continuous_subtype_val
      j_inj := fun v => e.injective.comp Subtype.val_injective
      j_range := fun v s => by
        refine ⟨?_, fun h => ⟨⟨e.symm s, by rw [hDec, he']; exact h⟩, e.apply_symm_apply s⟩⟩
        rintro ⟨t, rfl⟩
        exact he t.1 ▸ (hDec _ _).1 t.2
      j_loc := fun v s => (congrArg π (e.symm_apply_apply s.1)).symm
      j_ine := fun v s => (hIne _ s.1).trans (he s.1 ▸ Iff.rfl)
      dv := fun v => (deg_eq l (hA v) v.2).trans (by rw [upl_eq])
      mu := fun v => by
        obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := U.mu (A v) (hAl v)
        refine ⟨⟨e.symm ⟨s, hs.2⟩, by rw [hDec, he']; exact Ine_le_Dec _ hs.1⟩, ?_⟩
        exact (congrArg Subtype.val (e.apply_symm_apply ⟨s, hs.2⟩)).symm ▸ h }
  refine ⟨Φ, k, S, π, fun v => (Dec (F := W.Lp L) (A v)).subtype, Subtype.val, Equiv.refl _, c,
    ⟨inferInstance, GC.gLink_quot l g.odd (fun v hv => (hS v).2 hv) inferInstance hKo hKi, fun w hw => (hS w).1 hw,
      ⟨hA, Subtype.val_injective, fun w => ⟨?_, fun h => ⟨⟨w, h⟩, rfl⟩⟩, fun v => continuous_subtype_val,
        fun v => Subtype.val_injective, fun v => Subgroup.range_subtype _⟩,
      fun _ _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl, hcc, fun _ => rfl, ?_, hc, fun _ h => h.1, fun _ h => h.2⟩,
    fun s h => congrArg π ?_, fun v s => (he s.1).symm⟩
  · rintro ⟨v, rfl⟩
    exact v.2
  · exact (QuotientGroup.isQuotientMap_mk N).isOpen_preimage.1 (ho1.preimage hcont)
  · exact (e.symm_apply_eq.2 (Subtype.ext (he s).symm)).symm


[MFC_SRC_BASIC] Full original ok_basic conclusions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/OkBasic.lean#L46-L114
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.ok_basic :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (m : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)), UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L) → Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ → barChar O Ψ g₁ ^ 2 ≠ 1 → IsOpen (Φ.del.ker : Set Φ.Γ) ∧ Φ.c * Φ.c = 1 ∧ Φ.del Φ.c = -1 ∧ (∀ v, Continuous (Φ.loc0 v)) ∧ Continuous Φ.eps ∧ IsOpen (Φ.Ψ.ker : Set Φ.Γ) ∧ Φ.Ψ Φ.c = 1 ∧ Φ.Ψ Φ.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1 ∧ Fintype.card Φ.Inf = Φ.d ∧ (∀ w, Φ.cw w * Φ.cw w = 1) ∧ (∀ w, Φ.del (Φ.cw w) = -1) ∧ (∃ g : Φ.G0, Φ.om (ResidueField O) g ≠ 1) ∧ (∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : Φ.G0, Φ.om (ResidueField O) g * Φ.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1) ∧ (∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, Φ.om (ResidueField O) g * Φ.Ψ g ^ j * Φ.delK (ResidueField O) g ≠ 1) ∧ (∀ v, ∃ g, Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1) ∧ (¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : Φ.G0, Φ.om (ResidueField O) g = Φ.Ψ g ^ j) ∧ (∀ v, ∀ g ∈ Φ.Nv v, Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v g) = 1) ∧ (∀ v, l ^ m ≤ Φ.dv v) ∧ Nonempty Φ.V := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L _ _ _ _ g₁ Φ hU hL hg₁
  obtain ⟨k, S, π, ι, pl, e, c, F, hπ, -⟩ := hL
  haveI : CompactSpace (G (W.Lp L) Ω) := cpt_gal _ _
  have hq : Topology.IsQuotientMap π := F.g.cont.isClosedMap.isQuotientMap F.g.cont F.g.onto
  have hom : ∀ s, Φ.om (ResidueField O) (k.p s) = omO Ω l O s := fun s => by
    apply Units.ext
    show (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) (ResidueField O)).comp PadicInt.toZMod (Φ.eps (k.p s) : ℤ_[l]ˣ) =
      (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) (ResidueField O)).comp (PadicInt.toZModPow 1) (cycZ ℚ Ω l s : ℤ_[l]ˣ)
    rw [k.eps, padic_hom_ext ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) (ResidueField O)).comp PadicInt.toZMod)]
  have hpl : ∀ v, (k.pl v).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := fun v => by
    obtain ⟨γ, -, h⟩ := k.pl_conj v
    exact h ▸ smul_over γ hG.over_l
  have h0 : ∀ s (h : s ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker), k.p ⟨s, h.1⟩ ∈ Φ.del.ker := fun s h => (k.del _).trans h.2
  have f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : Φ.G0, Φ.om (ResidueField O) g = Φ.Ψ g ^ j := by
    rintro ⟨j, hj⟩
    refine hU.f5 ⟨j, fun s hs => ?_⟩
    have := hj ⟨_, h0 s hs⟩
    rwa [hom, k.psi] at this
  obtain ⟨hσ, hc⟩ := k.c
  obtain ⟨hg, hg0⟩ := k.g0
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hη⟩ := hG.quad
  refine ⟨F.del_open, ?_, F.del_c, fun v => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, fun w => ?_, fun w => ?_, ?_, fun j hj => ?_,
    fun j hj => ?_, fun v => ?_, f5, fun v g hg => ?_, fun v => ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.cc
    rw [← hc, ← map_mul, ← map_one k.p]
    exact congrArg k.p (Subtype.ext (cc_sq hj))
  · refine continuous_induced_rng.2 ?_
    have : Subtype.val ∘ Φ.loc0 v = k.p ∘ k.j v := funext (k.j_loc v)
    exact this ▸ k.p_cont.comp (k.j_cont v)
  · refine hq.continuous_iff.2 ?_
    have : Φ.eps ∘ π = cycZ (W.Lp L) Ω l := funext F.eps
    exact this ▸ continuous_cycZ _ _ l
  · exact hq.isOpen_preimage.1 (Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₂ := Φ.Ψ.ker.comap π) F.K_Ψ F.g.isOpen)
  · rw [← hc, k.psi]
    exact hG.even
  · rw [← hg0, k.psi]
    exact hg₁
  · classical
    haveI := F.real
    rw [Fintype.card_congr e, Frame.d, InfinitePlace.card_eq_nrRealPlaces_add_nrComplexPlaces,
      IsTotallyReal.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero, add_zero, ← IsTotallyReal.finrank,
      ← sum_locDegree l (Finset.univ.image pl) fun v => by
        rw [← F.loc.mem, Finset.mem_image]; exact ⟨fun ⟨a, _, h⟩ => ⟨a, h⟩, fun ⟨a, h⟩ => ⟨a, Finset.mem_univ _, h⟩⟩,
      Finset.sum_image fun a _ b _ h => F.loc.inj h]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun v _ => (F.dv v).symm
  · obtain ⟨j, hj, -⟩ := F.cc w
    rw [F.cw, ← map_mul, cc_sq hj, map_one]
  · obtain ⟨j, hj, -⟩ := F.cc w
    rw [F.cw, hπ _ fun x hx => (c w).commutes ⟨x, hx⟩, k.del]
    exact eta_cc hD hδ hη hj
  · by_contra h
    exact f5 ⟨0, fun g => by_contra fun h' => h ⟨g, by simpa using h'⟩⟩
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := hU.f2b j hj
    exact ⟨⟨_, h0 s hs⟩, by rwa [hom, k.psi]⟩
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := hU.f2c j hj
    refine ⟨k.p ⟨s, hs.1⟩, ?_⟩
    rwa [hom, k.psi, Frame.delK, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.mem_ker.1 (h0 s hs), map_one, mul_one]
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := hU.f3 _ (hpl v)
    obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := (k.j_range v ⟨s, hs.2⟩).2 hs.1
    exact ⟨g, by rwa [k.j_loc, k.psi, hg]⟩
  · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.Ψ_l
    have h1 : Φ.eps (Φ.loc v g) = 1 := hg.2
    have h2 : Φ.om (ResidueField O) (Φ.loc v g) = 1 := by rw [Frame.om, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1, map_one]
    rw [k.j_loc, hom] at h2
    rw [k.j_loc, k.psi, hj _ (hpl v) _ ((k.j_ine v g).1 hg.1), h2, one_pow]
  · exact k.dv v ▸ hU.deg _ (hpl v)
  · exact ⟨(k.pl_all B hG.over_l).exists.choose⟩


[MFC_SRC_ST_SEL] Full st_sel and actual global/local quotient transport
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/StSel.lean#L1-L128
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StCount
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.DualNontriv
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StDual
