open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem toGS_eq_one_of_mem_inertia_of_notMem (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))
    (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)) (hv : v ∉ S) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : IsPlaceOver A v)
    (s : G ℚ Ω) (hs : s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) : toGS Ω S s = 1 := by
  unfold toGS
  rw [QuotientGroup.mk'_apply, QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff]
  unfold ReciprocityTheory.Gal.NS
  apply Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure
  apply Subgroup.subset_normalClosure
  simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq, Set.mem_compl_iff]
  exact ⟨v, hv, A, hA, hs⟩

private theorem continuous_toGS' (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) :
    Continuous (toGS Ω S) := by
  unfold toGS
  exact continuous_quot_mk

private theorem toGS_surjective' (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) :
    Function.Surjective (toGS Ω S) := by
  unfold toGS
  exact QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective _

set_option maxHeartbeats 200000 in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_sel :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (κ : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ), IsOpen (κ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → (∃ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ ∧ κ σ ≠ 1) → (∃ s, κ s ≠ GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k s) → ∀ S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)), ContainsAbove S l → (∀ v ∉ S, ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker) → ∀ v₀ ∈ S, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∉ v₀.asIdeal → (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v₀ → Dec (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker ⊓ (GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k).ker) → (∀ v ∈ S, v ≠ v₀ → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, κ s ≠ 1) → ∃ y : G ℚ Ω → k, IsLocallyConstant y ∧ (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) ∧ (¬ ∃ b, ∀ s, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧ (∀ v ∉ S, ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, y s = 0) ∧ ∀ v ∈ S, v ≠ v₀ → ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v ∧ ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, y s = κ s * b - b := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ l _ hl2 k _ _ _ κ hκo hCC hs0ex S hSA hIne v₀ hv₀ hlv₀ hD0 hDo
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ, hσκ⟩ := hCC
  obtain ⟨s0, hs0⟩ := hs0ex
  have hdvd : l ∣ l ^ 1 := by simp
  haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by simpa using (Fact.out : l.Prime)⟩
  letI alg : Algebra (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k := (ZMod.castHom hdvd k).toAlgebra
  letI : TopologicalSpace k := ⊥
  haveI : DiscreteTopology k := ⟨rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨κS, hκS, hκSlc⟩ := st_desc Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) kˣ κ hκo
    (fun v hv A hA => hIne v hv A hA)
  have hκS' : ∀ g, κS (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) g) = κ g := fun g => by
    rw [← MonoidHom.comp_apply, hκS]
  let ρ : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))) k :=
    (Algebra.lmul (ZMod (l ^ 1)) k).toRingHom.toMonoidHom.comp ((Units.coeHom k).comp κS)
  have hρ : ∀ g m, ρ g m = (κS g : k) * m := fun g m => rfl
  have hcont : ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction ρ := by
    unfold ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction
    have hc : Continuous fun g => (κS g : k) :=
      (hκSlc.comp (fun u : kˣ => (u : k))).continuous
    exact (hc.comp continuous_fst).mul continuous_snd
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := GlobalTheory.Published.PoitouTate.exists_lift_cycMod (F := ℚ) (Ω := Ω) (l := l) (r := 1) hSA
  let Av : S → ValuationSubring Ω := fun v => (exists_isPlaceOver Ω v.1).choose
  have hAv : ∀ v : S, IsPlaceOver (Av v) v.1 := fun v => (exists_isPlaceOver Ω v.1).choose_spec
  have kill : ∀ (a : kˣ) (m : k), a ≠ 1 → (a : k) * m = m → m = 0 := by
    intro a m ha hm
    have h2 : ((a : k) - 1) * m = 0 := by rw [sub_mul, one_mul, hm, sub_self]
    exact (mul_eq_zero.mp h2).resolve_left (sub_ne_zero.mpr (fun h => ha (Units.val_eq_one.mp h)))
  have hfix : ∀ m : k, ρ (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) σ) m = m → m = 0 := by
    intro m hm
    rw [hρ, hκS'] at hm
    exact kill _ m hσκ hm
  have hdual : H0 (twDual μ ρ) = ⊥ := by
    refine st_dual l k _ μ ρ (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) s0) (κ s0 : k) (fun m => by rw [hρ, hκS']) ?_
    intro h
    apply hs0
    apply Units.ext
    have hμs : μ (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) s0) = cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s0 := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.comp_apply, hμ]
    rw [h, hμs]
    rfl
  have hother : ∀ v : S, v ≠ ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩ → H0 (MonoidHom.comp ρ (locFam S Av v)) = ⊥ := by
    intro v hv
    ext m
    rw [mem_riemannRochSpace_iff_regularAt_everywhere, Submodule.mem_bot]
    constructor
    · intro hm
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hκs⟩ := hDo v.1 v.2 (fun h => hv (Subtype.ext h)) (Av v) (hAv v)
      have h1 := hm ⟨s, hs⟩
      change (κS (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) s) : k) * m = m at h1
      rw [hκS'] at h1
      exact kill _ m hκs h1
    · rintro rfl g
      exact map_zero _
  have hloc : Nat.card (H0 (MonoidHom.comp ρ (locFam S Av ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩))) <
      Nat.card (H1 (MonoidHom.comp ρ (locFam S Av ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩))) := by
    refine st_loc hP.1.lf hP.1.lt hP.1.le Ω l k (Av ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) v₀ (hAv ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) hlv₀ ?_ _ ?_
    · intro g hg
      have h1 : GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k g = 1 := (hD0 (Av ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) (hAv ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) hg).2
      apply Units.ext
      apply (ZMod.castHom hdvd k).injective
      have h2 := congrArg Units.val h1
      rw [Units.val_one, map_one]
      exact h2
    · intro g m
      change (κS (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) g.1) : k) * m = m
      have h1 : κ g.1 = 1 := (hD0 (Av ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) (hAv ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩) g.2).1
      rw [hκS', h1, Units.val_one, one_mul]
  obtain ⟨z, hz, hzB, hzloc⟩ := st_count hP.1.gf hP.gw Ω l hl2 k S ρ hSA hcont μ hμ Av hAv σ hσ hfix
    hdual ⟨v₀, hv₀⟩ hother hloc
  rw [mem_Z1_iff_cocycle] at hz
  obtain ⟨hzc, hzco⟩ := hz
  have hz1 : z 1 = 0 := by
    have h1 := hzco 1 1
    rw [mul_one, hρ, map_one, Units.val_one, one_mul] at h1
    linear_combination (-1 : k) * h1
  refine ⟨fun s => z (toGS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) s), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact ((IsLocallyConstant.iff_continuous z).2 hzc).comp_continuous (continuous_toGS' Ω _)
  · intro s t
    simp only [map_mul]
    rw [hzco, hρ, hκS']
  · rintro ⟨b, hb⟩
    apply hzB
    rw [mem_coboundaries1]
    refine ⟨b, fun g => ?_⟩
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := toGS_surjective' Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) g
    have hbs := hb s
    beta_reduce at hbs
    rw [hbs, hρ, hκS']
  · intro v hv A hA s hs
    simp only
    rw [toGS_eq_one_of_mem_inertia_of_notMem Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) v hv A hA s hs, hz1]
  · intro v hv hne
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hzloc ⟨v, hv⟩ (fun h => hne (congrArg Subtype.val h))
    exact ⟨Av ⟨v, hv⟩, hAv _, m, fun s hs => by
      beta_reduce
      rw [hm s hs, hρ, hκS']⟩


[MFC_SRC_ST_COUNT] Greenberg-Wiles cardinal construction of a nonzero class
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/StCount.lean#L1-L76
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.InertiaEigenvalues.TwistedDualEvalBidualApply

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem card_bot' {R N : Type} [Ring R] [AddCommGroup N] [Module R N] :
    Nat.card (⊥ : Submodule R N) = 1 := by
  exact Nat.card_unique

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_count :
    GlobalFiniteness → GreenbergWiles → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M] [Finite M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) (ρ : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)))) M), ContainsAbove S l → ContAction ρ → ∀ μ : GS Ω (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ))) →* (ZMod (l ^ 1))ˣ, μ.comp (toGS Ω _) = cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 → ∀ Av : S → ValuationSubring Ω, (∀ v, IsPlaceOver (Av v) v.1) → ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → (∀ m, ρ (toGS Ω _ σ) m = m → m = 0) → H0 (twDual μ ρ) = ⊥ → ∀ v₀ : S, (∀ v, v ≠ v₀ → H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v)) = ⊥) → Nat.card (H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v₀))) < Nat.card (H1 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v₀))) → ∃ z ∈ Z1 ρ, z ∉ B1 ρ ∧ ∀ v, v ≠ v₀ → ∃ m, ∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (Av v), z (toGS Ω _ g) = ρ (toGS Ω _ g) m - m := by
  intro hGF hGW Ω _ _ _ l _ hl2 M _ _ _ _ _ S ρ hS hρ μ hμ Av hAv σ hσ hfix hdual v₀ hloc hlt
  classical
  have hH0 : H0 ρ = ⊥ := by
    rw [eq_bot_iff]
    intro m hm
    change m ∈ ρ.invariants at hm
    exact (Submodule.mem_bot _).2 (hfix m ((Representation.mem_invariants ρ m).1 hm _))
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ : ∃ L : LocCond ρ (locFam S Av), ∀ v, L v = if v = v₀ then ⊤ else ⊥ :=
    ⟨fun v => if v = v₀ then ⊤ else ⊥, fun _ => rfl⟩
  have hcc : ∀ w : InfinitePlace ℚ, w.IsReal → IsCCAt w ((fun _ => σ) w) := by
    intro w _
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hσ
    exact ⟨j, hj, Subsingleton.elim _ _⟩
  have hgw := hGW ℚ Ω l 1 M S ρ one_pos hl2 hS hρ μ hμ Av hAv (fun _ => σ) hcc L
  obtain ⟨_, hfin1, _, _⟩ := hGF ℚ Ω l 1 M S ρ one_pos hS hρ
  have e1 : Nat.card (H0 (twDual μ ρ)) = 1 := by
    rw [hdual]
    exact card_bot'
  have e2 : ∏ v, Nat.card (H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v))) = Nat.card (H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v₀))) := by
    refine Finset.prod_eq_single v₀ (fun v _ hv => ?_) (fun h => absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h)
    rw [hloc v hv]
    exact card_bot'
  have e3 : archH0Card S ρ (fun _ => σ) = 1 := by
    unfold archH0Card
    rw [InfinitePlace.nrComplexPlaces_eq_zero_of_finrank_eq_one (Module.finrank_self ℚ), pow_zero, mul_one]
    apply Finset.prod_eq_one
    intro w _
    exact Nat.card_eq_one_iff_unique.2
      ⟨⟨fun a b => Subtype.ext ((hfix _ a.2).trans (hfix _ b.2).symm)⟩, ⟨⟨0, map_zero _⟩⟩⟩
  have e4 : Nat.card (H0 ρ) = 1 := by
    rw [hH0]
    exact card_bot'
  have e5 : ∏ v, Nat.card (L v) = Nat.card (H1 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v₀))) := by
    rw [Finset.prod_eq_single v₀ (fun v _ hv => by rw [hL, if_neg hv]; exact card_bot')
      (fun h => absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h)]
    rw [hL, if_pos rfl]
    exact Nat.card_congr Submodule.topEquiv.toEquiv
  rw [e1, e2, e3, e4, e5] at hgw
  have hapos : 0 < Nat.card (Sel ρ (locFam S Av) (continuous_locFam S Av) L) := Nat.card_pos
  have hcpos : 0 < Nat.card (H0 (ρ.comp (locFam S Av v₀))) := Nat.card_pos
  have key : ∀ a b c d : ℕ, a * 1 * c * 1 = b * 1 * d → c < d → 0 < a → 0 < c → 1 < a := by
    intro a b c d h hcd ha hc
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos b with rfl | hb
    · simp at h
      omega
    · by_contra hle
      have ha1 : a = 1 := by omega
      subst ha1
      nlinarith
  have h2 := key _ _ _ _ hgw hlt hapos hcpos
  haveI := Finite.one_lt_card_iff_nontrivial.1 h2
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_ne (0 : Sel ρ (locFam S Av) (continuous_locFam S Av) L)
  obtain ⟨z', hz'⟩ := Submodule.Quotient.mk_surjective _ x.1
  refine ⟨z'.1, z'.2, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro hz
    apply hx
    apply Subtype.ext
    rw [Submodule.coe_zero, ← hz']
    exact (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 hz
  · intro v hv
    have hm := (mem_Sel ρ (locFam S Av) (continuous_locFam S Av) L x.1).1 x.2 v
    rw [hL, if_neg hv, Submodule.mem_bot, ← hz'] at hm
    simp only [resH1, Submodule.mapQ_apply] at hm
    obtain ⟨m, hm'⟩ := (mem_coboundaries1 _ _).1 ((Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).1 hm)
    exact ⟨m, fun g hg => hm' ⟨g, hg⟩⟩


[MFC_SRC_ST_LOCAL] Local cohomology cardinal strict inequality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/DualNontriv.lean#L1-L55
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem dual_nontriv (K V : Type) [Field K] [AddCommGroup V] [Module K V] [Nontrivial V] :
    Nontrivial (Module.Dual K V) := by
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := exists_ne (0 : V)
  by_contra h
  rw [not_nontrivial_iff_subsingleton] at h
  exact hv ((Module.forall_dual_apply_eq_zero_iff K v).mp fun φ => by
    rw [Subsingleton.elim φ 0]
    rfl)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_loc :
    LocalFiniteness → LocalTateDuality → LocalEulerChar → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (M : Type) [AddCommGroup M] [Module (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M] [Finite M] [TopologicalSpace M] [DiscreteTopology M] [Nontrivial M] (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)), IsPlaceOver A v → ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∉ v.asIdeal → (∀ g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 g = 1) → ∀ ρ : Representation (ZMod (l ^ 1)) (Dec (F := ℚ) A) M, (∀ g m, ρ g m = m) → Nat.card (H0 ρ) < Nat.card (H1 ρ) := by
  intro hFin hTate hEuler Ω _ _ _ l hl M _ _ _ _ _ _ A v hv hlv hcyc ρ htriv
  haveI : Fact (l ^ 1).Prime := ⟨by rw [pow_one]; exact hl.out⟩
  have hcont : ReciprocityTheory.Gal.ContAction ρ := by
    show Continuous fun p : Dec (F := ℚ) A × M => ρ p.1 p.2
    simp only [htriv]
    exact continuous_snd
  have hF := hFin ℚ Ω l 1 M A ρ v one_pos hv hcont
  have hE := (hEuler ℚ Ω l 1 M A ρ v one_pos hv hcont).2 hlv
  obtain ⟨⟨e⟩, -, -, -, hbij⟩ := hTate ℚ Ω l 1 M A ρ v one_pos hv hcont
  haveI : Finite (H2 ρ) := hF.2.2.1
  haveI : Finite (H0 ρ) := hF.1
  have hinv : ∀ φ : TDual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M,
      φ ∈ H0 ((twDual ((cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) A).subtype) ρ).comp (MonoidHom.id _)) := by
    intro φ g
    apply TDual.ext
    intro m
    simp [twDual, hcyc g.1 g.2]
    show φ (ρ g⁻¹ m) = φ m
    rw [htriv]
  haveI : Nontrivial (TDual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M) := dual_nontriv (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M
  obtain ⟨φ0, hφ0⟩ := exists_ne (0 : TDual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) M)
  set X := H0 ((twDual ((cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) A).subtype) ρ).comp (MonoidHom.id _))
  have hx0 : (⟨φ0, hinv φ0⟩ : X) ≠ 0 := fun h => hφ0 (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ : ∃ f : Module.Dual (ZMod (l ^ 1)) X, f ⟨φ0, hinv φ0⟩ ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    exact hx0 ((Module.forall_dual_apply_eq_zero_iff (ZMod (l ^ 1)) _).mp h)
  let ψ : X →ₗ[ZMod (l ^ 1)] H2 ((charRep ((cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).comp (Dec (F := ℚ) A).subtype)).comp
      (MonoidHom.id _)) := e.symm.toLinearMap ∘ₗ f
  have hψ : ψ ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have h1 : ψ ⟨φ0, hinv φ0⟩ = 0 := by rw [h]; rfl
    exact hf (e.symm.map_eq_zero_iff.mp h1)
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hbij.2 ψ
  have hy0 : y ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    exact hψ (by rw [← hy, map_zero])
  haveI : Nontrivial (H2 ρ) := ⟨⟨y, 0, hy0⟩⟩
  rw [hE]
  exact lt_mul_of_one_lt_right (Nat.card_pos) Finite.one_lt_card


[MFC_SRC_ST_CONJ] Exact conjugate local coboundary primitive
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/StConj.lean#L1-L22
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V
open scoped Pointwise

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_conj :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (k : Type) [Field k] (κ : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ) (y : G ℚ Ω → k), (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) → ∀ (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)) (A A' : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A v → IsPlaceOver A' v → (∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, y s = κ s * b - b) → ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A', y s = κ s * b - b := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ k _ κ y hy v A A' hA hA' ⟨b, hb⟩
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := exists_smul_eq_of_isPlaceOver (F := ℚ) hA hA'
  refine ⟨(κ σ : k) * b - y σ, fun s hs => ?_⟩
  have hs' : s • A' = A' := mem_Dec.mp hs
  have ht : σ⁻¹ * s * σ ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A := by
    rw [mem_Dec, mul_smul, mul_smul, hσ, hs', ← hσ, ← mul_smul, inv_mul_cancel, one_smul]
  have hbt := hb _ ht
  have hκ : κ (σ⁻¹ * s * σ) = κ s := by
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm, ← mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel, one_mul]
  have hκ' : ((κ (σ⁻¹ * s * σ) : kˣ) : k) = κ s := congrArg Units.val hκ
  have h1 := hy s σ
  have h2 := hy σ (σ⁻¹ * s * σ)
  have h3 : σ * (σ⁻¹ * s * σ) = s * σ := by group
  rw [h3] at h2
  linear_combination -h1 + h2 + (κ σ : k) * hbt + (κ σ : k) * b * hκ'


[MFC_SRC_ST_RES] Restriction detects a global coboundary
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/StRes.lean#L1-L31
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_res :
    ∀ (Γ k : Type) [Group Γ] [Field k] (κ : Γ →* kˣ) (U : Subgroup Γ), commutator Γ ≤ U → (∃ u ∈ U, κ u ≠ 1) → ∀ y : Γ → k, (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) → (∃ b, ∀ s ∈ U, y s = κ s * b - b) → ∃ b, ∀ s, y s = κ s * b - b := by
  intro Γ k _ _ κ U hU hu y hy hb
  obtain ⟨u, huU, hu1⟩ := hu
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hb
  refine ⟨b, fun s => ?_⟩
  have hc : s * u * s⁻¹ * u⁻¹ ∈ U := by
    apply hU
    have := Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top s) (Subgroup.mem_top u)
    rw [commutatorElement_def] at this
    exact this
  have hv : s * u * s⁻¹ ∈ U := by
    have := U.mul_mem hc huU
    simpa using this
  have hκ : κ (s * u * s⁻¹) = κ u := by
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm (κ s) (κ u), mul_inv_cancel_right]
  have h1 := hy s u
  have h2 := hy (s * u * s⁻¹) s
  rw [inv_mul_cancel_right, hb _ hv, hκ] at h2
  rw [hb u huU] at h1
  have hne : ((κ u : k) - 1) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    apply hu1
    ext
    simpa using sub_eq_zero.mp h
  apply mul_left_cancel₀ hne
  linear_combination h1 - h2


[MFC_SRC_ST_TAME] Tame inertia and the index-ell kernel
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/StTame.lean#L1-L79
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.QuadraticExtensions.TameGen

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem exists_char_of_index {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (H : Subgroup Γ) (ho : IsOpen (H : Set Γ)) (hc : commutator Γ ≤ H)
    (hi : H.index = l) :
    ∃ φ : Γ → ZMod l, IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ (∀ a b, φ (a * b) = φ a + φ b) ∧ ∀ a, φ a = 0 ↔ a ∈ H := by
  have hN : H.Normal := by
    refine ⟨fun a ha g => ?_⟩
    have hc' : g * a * g⁻¹ * a⁻¹ ∈ H :=
      hc (Subgroup.commutator_mem_commutator (Subgroup.mem_top g) (Subgroup.mem_top a))
    have h2 := H.mul_mem hc' ha
    simpa using h2
  have hQ : Nat.card (Γ ⧸ H) = l := hi
  have hM : Nat.card (Multiplicative (ZMod l)) = l := by
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
    simp
  haveI : IsCyclic (Γ ⧸ H) := isCyclic_of_prime_card hQ
  haveI : IsCyclic (Multiplicative (ZMod l)) := isCyclic_of_prime_card hM
  let e : (Γ ⧸ H) ≃* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := mulEquivOfCyclicCardEq (hQ.trans hM.symm)
  let f : Γ →* Multiplicative (ZMod l) := (e : (Γ ⧸ H) →* Multiplicative (ZMod l)).comp (QuotientGroup.mk' H)
  have hadd : ∀ a b, Multiplicative.toAdd (f (a * b)) = Multiplicative.toAdd (f a) + Multiplicative.toAdd (f b) := by
    intro a b
    rw [map_mul, toAdd_mul]
  have hker : ∀ a, Multiplicative.toAdd (f a) = 0 ↔ a ∈ H := by
    intro a
    have h1 : Multiplicative.toAdd (f a) = 0 ↔ f a = 1 := by
      constructor
      · intro h
        exact Multiplicative.toAdd.injective (by simpa using h)
      · intro h
        rw [h]
        rfl
    rw [h1]
    simp only [f, MonoidHom.coe_comp, Function.comp_apply, QuotientGroup.mk'_apply, MonoidHom.coe_coe,
      MulEquiv.map_eq_one_iff, QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff]
  refine ⟨fun a => Multiplicative.toAdd (f a), ?_, hadd, hker⟩
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_exists_open]
  intro x
  refine ⟨(fun h => x * h) '' (H : Set Γ), isOpenMap_mul_left x _ ho, ⟨1, H.one_mem, mul_one x⟩, ?_⟩
  rintro _ ⟨h, hh, rfl⟩
  show Multiplicative.toAdd (f (x * h)) = Multiplicative.toAdd (f x)
  rw [hadd, (hker h).2 hh, add_zero]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.st_tame :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (A : ValuationSubring Ω), ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (H : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ H → H.index = l → ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ H → ∀ y : G ℚ Ω → k, IsLocallyConstant y → (∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, ∀ t ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, y (s * t) = y s + y t) → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ⊓ H, y s = 0 := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ k _ _ A hA H ho hc hi hIH y hy hyadd s hs
  obtain ⟨φ, hφc, hφa, hφk⟩ := exists_char_of_index l H ho hc hi
  obtain ⟨γ, hγ⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Two.tameGen ℚ Ω l A hA
  let φI : ↥(Ine (F := ℚ) A) → ZMod l := fun σ => φ σ
  have hφIc : IsLocallyConstant φI := hφc.comp_continuous continuous_subtype_val
  have hφIa : ∀ a b : ↥(Ine (F := ℚ) A), φI (a * b) = φI a + φI b := fun a b => hφa _ _
  have hγne : φI γ ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    apply hIH
    intro σ hσ
    exact (hφk σ).1 (hγ φI hφIc hφIa h0 ⟨σ, hσ⟩)
  letI : Algebra (ZMod l) k := ZMod.algebra k l
  rw [← Module.forall_dual_apply_eq_zero_iff (ZMod l)]
  intro μ
  let c : ZMod l := μ (y γ) / φI γ
  let ψ : ↥(Ine (F := ℚ) A) → ZMod l := fun σ => μ (y σ) - c * φI σ
  have hyI : IsLocallyConstant (fun σ : ↥(Ine (F := ℚ) A) => y σ) := hy.comp_continuous continuous_subtype_val
  have hψc : IsLocallyConstant ψ := IsLocallyConstant.comp₂ hyI hφIc (fun u v => μ u - c * v)
  have hψa : ∀ a b : ↥(Ine (F := ℚ) A), ψ (a * b) = ψ a + ψ b := by
    intro a b
    simp only [ψ]
    rw [hφIa, Subgroup.coe_mul, hyadd _ a.2 _ b.2, map_add]
    ring
  have hψγ : ψ γ = 0 := by
    simp only [ψ, c]
    rw [div_mul_cancel₀ _ hγne, sub_self]
  have h := hγ ψ hψc hψa hψγ ⟨s, hs.1⟩
  have h0 : φI ⟨s, hs.1⟩ = 0 := (hφk s).2 hs.2
  simp only [ψ] at h
  rw [h0, mul_zero, sub_zero] at h
  exact h


[MFC_SRC_STRICT] Full strict for both residual characters
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/Strict.lean#L1-L174
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Ufacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.HthPlace
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StSel
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.ExistsPlaceRat
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StConj
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StTame
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StRes
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StEta
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StMu
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PlaceDict

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem st_char : GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [Finite k] [CharP k l] (κ : G ℚ Ω →* kˣ), IsOpen (κ.ker : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → (∃ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ ∧ κ σ ≠ 1) → (∃ s, κ s ≠ GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k s) → ∀ N w : ℕ, N ≠ 0 → w.Prime → w ≠ l → (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker) → (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker ⊓ (GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k).ker) → (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, κ s ≠ 1) →
    ∃ y : G ℚ Ω → k, IsLocallyConstant y ∧ (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) ∧ (¬ ∃ b, ∀ s, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧ (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
      ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, κ s = 1 → y s = 0 := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ l _ hl2 k _ _ _ κ hκo hcc hcyc N w hN hw hwl hram hdecw hdecl
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨hIt, hex, hne, hdict⟩ := Kum.placeDict ℚ Ω
  obtain ⟨S, v₀, hv₀, hS⟩ := st_set Ω l w N hlp hw hN _ κ hram
  have hmem : ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)) (n : ℕ), IsPlaceOver A v →
      (A.valuation (n : Ω) < 1 ↔ (n : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal) :=
    fun A v n h => hdict A v n h
  have hw0 : (w : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v₀.asIdeal := (hv₀ v₀).2 rfl
  have hl_notin : (l : 𝓞 ℚ) ∉ v₀.asIdeal := by
    intro hl
    have hcop : IsCoprime (l : ℤ) (w : ℤ) :=
      Nat.isCoprime_iff_coprime.2 ((Nat.coprime_primes hlp hw).2 (Ne.symm hwl))
    have hcop' : IsCoprime (l : 𝓞 ℚ) (w : 𝓞 ℚ) := by
      simpa using hcop.map (Int.castRingHom (𝓞 ℚ))
    obtain ⟨u, u', hu⟩ := hcop'
    apply v₀.isPrime.ne_top
    rw [Ideal.eq_top_iff_one, ← hu]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hl) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hw0)
  have hv₀S : v₀ ∈ S := (hS v₀ (placeOf Ω v₀) (isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω v₀)).2 (Or.inr (Or.inl rfl))
  have hCA : ContainsAbove S l := fun v hv => (hS v (placeOf Ω v) (isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω v)).2 (Or.inl hv)
  have hout : ∀ v ∉ S, ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker := by
    intro v hv A hA
    by_contra h
    exact hv ((hS v A hA).2 (Or.inr (Or.inr h)))
  have hdec0 : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v₀ → Dec (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker ⊓ (GC.cycF ℚ Ω l k).ker :=
    fun A hA => hdecw A ((hmem A v₀ w hA).2 hw0)
  have hother : ∀ v ∈ S, v ≠ v₀ → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, κ s ≠ 1 := by
    intro v hv hne' A hA
    rcases (hS v A hA).1 hv with h | h | h
    · exact hdecl A ((hmem A v l hA).2 h)
    · exact absurd h hne'
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hs'⟩ := SetLike.not_le_iff_exists.1 h
      exact ⟨s, Ine_le_Dec A hs, by rwa [MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hs'⟩
  obtain ⟨y, hyc, hyco, hynot, hyout, hyS⟩ :=
    st_sel hP Ω l hl2 k κ hκo hcc hcyc S hCA hout v₀ hv₀S hl_notin hdec0 hother
  have hcob : ∀ v ∈ S, v ≠ v₀ → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v →
      ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, y s = (κ s : k) * b - b := by
    intro v hv hne' A hA
    obtain ⟨A0, hA0, hb⟩ := hyS v hv hne'
    exact st_conj Ω k κ y hyco v A0 A hA0 hA hb
  refine ⟨y, hyc, hyco, hynot, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro A hA
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hex A (hne A l hlp.ne_zero hA)
    have hlv := (hmem A v l hv).1 hA
    refine hcob v (hCA v hlv) ?_ A hv
    rintro rfl
    exact hl_notin hlv
  · intro A hA s hs hκs
    by_cases htop : A = ⊤
    · subst htop
      rw [hIt] at hs
      rw [Subgroup.mem_bot.1 hs]
      have h := hyco 1 1
      rw [mul_one, map_one, Units.val_one, one_mul] at h
      simpa using h
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hex A htop
    by_cases hvS : v ∈ S
    · have hvne : v ≠ v₀ := by
        intro h
        exact hA ((hmem A v w hv).2 (h ▸ hw0))
      obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hcob v hvS hvne A hv
      rw [hb s (Ine_le_Dec A hs), hκs, Units.val_one, one_mul, sub_self]
    · exact hyout v hvS A hv s hs

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.strict :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w : ℕ), FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum η M H₀ → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → ∀ (m : ℕ) (a : ℕ), a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (Upl l Hb H₀ w m) := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m a ha
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField O) := hG.fin
  have hF := ufacts l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m
  set U := Upl l Hb H₀ w m with hU
  set κ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := (barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η with hκdef
  have hκ : ∀ s, κ s = ((barChar O Ψ s)⁻¹) ^ a * etaO O η s := fun s => rfl
  have hκη : ∀ s, η s = 1 → κ s = ((barChar O Ψ s)⁻¹) ^ a := by
    intro s h1
    rw [hκ]
    show _ * Units.map _ (η s) = _
    rw [h1, map_one, mul_one]
  have hκ1 : ∀ s, η s = 1 → barChar O Ψ s = 1 → κ s = 1 := by
    intro s h1 h2
    rw [hκη s h1, h2, inv_one, one_pow]
  obtain ⟨b, hηb, hΨb⟩ := hG.cyc
  have hκK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ b) κ := fun s hs => hκ1 s (hηb s hs) (hΨb s hs)
  obtain ⟨hKo, hram⟩ := st_mu Ω M b hG.M_ne _ κ hκK
  have hM_l : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → A.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := by
    intro A hA
    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    rw [ht, Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' (valuation_natCast_le_one A t)) hA
  have hdecl : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A ⊓ U, κ s ≠ 1 := by
    intro A hA
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hs2⟩ := hF.f3 A hA
    have hη : η s = 1 := hF.split A (hM_l A hA) hs
    refine ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
    rw [hκη s hη]
    rcases ha with rfl | rfl
    · intro h
      apply hs2
      rw [pow_one, inv_eq_one] at h
      rw [h, one_pow]
    · intro h
      apply hs2
      rwa [inv_pow, inv_eq_one] at h
  have hne1 : (-1 : ResidueField O) ≠ 1 := by
    rw [Ne, neg_one_eq_one_iff, ringChar.eq (ResidueField O) l]
    exact hG.odd
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hηδ⟩ := hG.quad
  have hησ : η σ = -1 := st_eta Ω η D δ hD hδ hηδ σ hG.cc
  have hκσ : κ σ ≠ 1 := by
    rw [hκ, hG.even, inv_one, one_pow, one_mul]
    intro h
    apply hne1
    have h' := congrArg (fun u : (ResidueField O)ˣ => (u : ResidueField O)) h
    simpa [etaO, hησ] using h'
  have hcyc : ∃ s, κ s ≠ GC.cycF ℚ Ω l (ResidueField O) s := by
    have hex : ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, (omO Ω l O) s * (barChar O Ψ) s ^ (a : ℤ) ≠ 1 := by
      rcases ha with rfl | rfl
      · exact hF.f2b 1 (Or.inl rfl)
      · exact hF.f2c 2 (Or.inl rfl)
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := hex
    have hη : η s = 1 := (Subgroup.mem_inf.1 hs).2
    refine ⟨s, fun h => hne ?_⟩
    have h' : omO Ω l O s = κ s := h.symm
    rw [h', hκη s hη, zpow_natCast, inv_pow, inv_mul_cancel]
  have hwl : w ≠ l := by
    intro h
    have h1 : w % l = 1 % l := hw.one
    rw [h, Nat.mod_self, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt hlp.one_lt] at h1
    exact zero_ne_one h1
  have hdecw : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 →
      Dec (F := ℚ) A ≤ κ.ker ⊓ (GC.cycF ℚ Ω l (ResidueField O)).ker := by
    intro A hA s hs
    obtain ⟨h12, h3⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.1 (hw.split A hA hs)
    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.1 h12
    exact Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨hκ1 s h1 h2, h3⟩
  obtain ⟨y, hyc, hyco, hynot, hydec, hyw⟩ :=
    st_char hP Ω l hG.odd (ResidueField O) κ hKo ⟨σ, hG.cc, hκσ⟩ hcyc M w hG.M_ne hw.prime hwl hram hdecw
      (fun A hA => by
        obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := hdecl A hA
        exact ⟨s, (Subgroup.mem_inf.1 hs).1, h⟩)
  refine ⟨y, hyc, hyco, ?_, hydec, ?_⟩
  · rintro ⟨b0, hb0⟩
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hκs⟩ := hdecl B hG.over_l
    exact hynot (st_res (G ℚ Ω) _ κ U hF.ab ⟨s, (Subgroup.mem_inf.1 hs).2, hκs⟩ y hyco ⟨b0, hb0⟩)
  · intro B' hB' s hs hη hΨ
    have hs' := Subgroup.mem_inf.1 hs
    by_cases hBw : B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1
    · have hth' := hth Ω l w hw.prime hw.one
      have hsH : s ∈ Hth Ω l w := by
        have h2 := hs'.2
        simp only [hU, Upl, Subgroup.mem_inf] at h2
        exact h2.1.1.2
      refine st_tame Ω l (ResidueField O) B' hB' _ hth'.1 hth'.2.1 hth'.2.2.1 (hth'.2.2.2.2 B' hBw) y hyc ?_ s
        (Subgroup.mem_inf.2 ⟨hs'.1, hsH⟩)
      intro s1 hs1 t1 _
      have hk : κ s1 = 1 := (Subgroup.mem_inf.1 (hdecw B' hBw (Ine_le_Dec B' hs1))).1
      rw [hyco, hk, Units.val_one, one_mul]
    · exact hyw B' hBw s hs'.1 (hκ1 s hη hΨ)


[MFC_SRC_F4] Exact strict-cocycle descent to the original cone
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/FrobeniusGaloisFieldFourCompatibility.lean#L1-L160

private theorem smul_val_lt {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] (g : G ℚ Ω) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (n : ℕ)
    (hA : A.valuation (n : Ω) < 1) : (g • A).valuation (n : Ω) < 1 := by
  have fix : ∀ y : Ω, g y = y → (y ∈ g • A ↔ y ∈ A) := by
    intro y hy
    have hy' : g • y = y := hy
    constructor
    · intro h
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hgs⟩ := (ValuationSubring.mem_smul_pointwise_iff_exists g y A).mp h
      have : s = y := by
        rw [← inv_smul_smul g s, hgs, inv_smul_eq_iff, hy']
      rwa [this] at hs
    · intro h
      have := ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul g y A h
      rwa [hy'] at this
  have vlt : ∀ (C : ValuationSubring Ω) (x : Ω), x ≠ 0 → (C.valuation x < 1 ↔ x ∈ C ∧ x⁻¹ ∉ C) := by
    intro C x hx
    haveI : Nontrivial C.ValueGroup := GroupWithZero.toNontrivial
    rw [← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, ← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, map_inv₀,
      inv_le_one₀ ((Valuation.pos_iff _).mpr hx), lt_iff_le_not_ge]
  by_cases h0 : (n : Ω) = 0
  · rw [h0, map_zero]
    haveI : Nontrivial (g • A).ValueGroup := GroupWithZero.toNontrivial
    exact zero_lt_one
  · have hn : g (n : Ω) = n := map_natCast g n
    have hni : g ((n : Ω)⁻¹) = (n : Ω)⁻¹ := by rw [map_inv₀, hn]
    rw [vlt _ _ h0] at hA ⊢
    rw [fix _ hn, fix _ hni]
    exact hA

private theorem descend {U Γ k : Type} [Group U] [TopologicalSpace U] [IsTopologicalGroup U] [CompactSpace U]
    [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ] [CommRing k] (p : U →* Γ) (hc : Continuous p)
    (hs : Function.Surjective p) (S : Set U) (hk : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure S).topologicalClosure)
    (χ : Γ →* kˣ) (y : U → k) (hl : IsLocallyConstant y) (hy : ∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + χ (p s) * y t)
    (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, y s = 0) :
    ∃ φ : Γ → k, IsLocallyConstant φ ∧ ∀ s, φ (p s) = y s := by
  have hy1 : y 1 = 0 := by
    have e := hy 1 1
    rw [mul_one, map_one, map_one, Units.val_one, one_mul] at e
    linear_combination -e
  let H : Subgroup U :=
    { carrier := {t | p t = 1 ∧ y t = 0}
      one_mem' := ⟨map_one p, hy1⟩
      mul_mem' := by
        rintro a b ⟨ha1, ha2⟩ ⟨hb1, hb2⟩
        show p (a * b) = 1 ∧ y (a * b) = 0
        refine ⟨by rw [map_mul, ha1, hb1, one_mul], ?_⟩
        rw [hy, ha2, hb2, mul_zero, add_zero]
      inv_mem' := by
        rintro a ⟨ha1, ha2⟩
        show p a⁻¹ = 1 ∧ y a⁻¹ = 0
        refine ⟨by rw [map_inv, ha1, inv_one], ?_⟩
        have e := hy a a⁻¹
        rw [mul_inv_cancel, hy1, ha2, ha1, map_one, Units.val_one, one_mul, zero_add] at e
        exact e.symm }
  have hH : H.Normal := ⟨fun t ht g => by
    obtain ⟨ht1, ht2⟩ := ht
    show p (g * t * g⁻¹) = 1 ∧ y (g * t * g⁻¹) = 0
    refine ⟨by rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, ht1, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel], ?_⟩
    have e1 := hy (g * t) g⁻¹
    have e2 := hy g t
    have e3 := hy g g⁻¹
    rw [mul_inv_cancel, hy1] at e3
    rw [map_mul, ht1, mul_one] at e1
    rw [ht2, mul_zero, add_zero] at e2
    rw [e1, e2, ← e3]⟩
  have hcl : IsClosed (H : Set U) :=
    (isClosed_eq hc continuous_const).inter (hl.isClosed_fiber 0)
  have hKH : p.ker ≤ H := by
    rw [hk]
    apply Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ _ hcl
    haveI := hH
    apply Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal
    intro s hsS
    have hps : p s = 1 := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, hk]
      exact Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure hsS)
    exact ⟨hps, hS s hsS⟩
  have hfib : ∀ s t, p s = p t → y s = y t := by
    intro s t hst
    have hk' : s⁻¹ * t ∈ p.ker := by rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, map_inv, hst, inv_mul_cancel]
    obtain ⟨_, h2⟩ := hKH hk'
    have e := hy s (s⁻¹ * t)
    rw [mul_inv_cancel_left, h2, mul_zero, add_zero] at e
    exact e.symm
  have hq : Topology.IsQuotientMap p := (hc.isClosedMap).isQuotientMap hc hs
  refine ⟨fun γ => y (Function.surjInv hs γ), fun V => ?_, fun s => hfib _ _ (Function.surjInv_eq hs (p s))⟩
  have e : p ⁻¹' ((fun γ => y (Function.surjInv hs γ)) ⁻¹' V) = y ⁻¹' V := by
    ext s
    simp only [Set.mem_preimage]
    rw [hfib _ _ (Function.surjInv_eq hs (p s))]
  apply hq.isOpen_preimage.mp
  rw [e]
  exact hl V

end UpperTheory.V.Fr.DescentValuationAlt

open UpperTheory.V.Fr.DescentValuationAlt in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ (m : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)), UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L) → Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ → ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L) → Nontrivial (H1C Φ.loc (chRep (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O))) fun _ => ⊥) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L _ _ _ _ g₁ Φ hU hL a ha hS
  obtain ⟨k, -⟩ := hL
  obtain ⟨y, hyl, hyc, hyn, hyd, hyi⟩ := hS
  have hχ : ∀ s : upl Ω L, (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O)) (k.p s)
      = ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) s := by
    intro s
    rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply,
      MonoidHom.inv_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, k.psi]
    congr 1
    simp only [Frame.delK, etaO, MonoidHom.comp_apply, k.del]
  have hUc : IsClosed (upl Ω L : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := Subgroup.isClosed_of_isOpen _ hU.isOpen
  haveI : CompactSpace (upl Ω L) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.mp hUc.isCompact
  obtain ⟨φ, hφl, hφ⟩ := descend k.p k.p_cont k.p_surj _ k.p_ker (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O))
    (fun s => y s) (hyl.comp_continuous continuous_subtype_val)
    (fun s t => by rw [hχ s]; exact hyc s t)
    (fun s hs => by
      obtain ⟨B', hB', hI, hη, hb⟩ := hs
      exact hyi B' hB' s (Subgroup.mem_inf.mpr ⟨hI, s.2⟩) hη hb)
  have hρ : ∀ (γ : Φ.Γ) (w : ResidueField O),
      (chRep (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O)) γ) w
        = ((Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O)) γ : ResidueField O) * w := fun γ w => rfl
  have hcoc : ∀ γ δ : Φ.Γ, φ (γ * δ)
      = φ γ + ((Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O)) γ : ResidueField O) * φ δ := by
    intro γ δ
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := k.p_surj γ
    obtain ⟨t, rfl⟩ := k.p_surj δ
    rw [← map_mul, hφ, hφ, hφ, hχ]
    exact hyc s t
  have hloc : ∀ v : Φ.V, ∃ b : ResidueField O, ∀ g : Φ.Gv v,
      φ (Φ.loc v g) = ((Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O)) (Φ.loc v g) : ResidueField O) * b - b := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨γ, -, hγ⟩ := k.pl_conj v
    have hv : (k.pl v).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := by
      rw [hγ]
      exact smul_val_lt γ B l hG.over_l
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hyd _ hv
    refine ⟨b, fun g => ?_⟩
    rw [k.j_loc, hφ, hχ]
    exact hb _ ((k.j_range v _).mp (MonoidHom.mem_range.mpr ⟨g, rfl⟩))
  choose b hb using hloc
  let z : Z1C Φ.loc (chRep (Φ.Ψ⁻¹ ^ a * Φ.delK (ResidueField O))) (fun _ => ⊥) :=
    ⟨(φ, b), ⟨hφl, fun γ δ => by rw [hρ]; exact hcoc γ δ,
      fun v g => by rw [Submodule.mem_bot, hρ, ← hb v g, sub_self]⟩⟩
  refine ⟨⟨Submodule.Quotient.mk z, 0, fun h => ?_⟩⟩
  have hz := (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).mp h
  rw [Submodule.mem_comap] at hz
  obtain ⟨X, hX1, -⟩ := hz
  apply hyn
  refine ⟨X, fun s hs => ?_⟩
  have e1 := hφ ⟨s, hs⟩
  have e2 := hX1 (k.p ⟨s, hs⟩)
  rw [hρ, hχ] at e2
  rw [← e2]
  exact e1.symm

private theorem cycMod_emb (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (g : G F Ω) : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (galEmb ℚ F Ω g) = cycMod F Ω l 1 g := rfl

private theorem one_of_cast (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (x : ℤ_[l])
    (h : ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k (PadicInt.toZMod x) = 1) : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x = 1 := by
  have h1 : x - 1 ∈ RingHom.ker (PadicInt.toZMod : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod l) := by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
    exact (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k).injective (h.trans (map_one _).symm)
  rw [PadicInt.ker_toZMod, PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, ← pow_one (l : ℤ_[l]), ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow,
    RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero] at h1
  exact h1

private theorem rk_link {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (A A' : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) :
    GC.clRk l ((A ⊓ A').comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω) ⊓ (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) =
      GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) := by
  have hU : upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ≤ galOf ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    fun g hg x => hg.1.1.1 x.1 x.2
  rw [← clrk_tr Ω l (W.Lp L) _ hU]
  congr 1
  ext g
  simp only [Subgroup.mem_inf, Subgroup.mem_comap, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
  rw [cycMod_emb]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨⟨fun x hx => galEmb_mem ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ⟨x, hx⟩, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
  · rintro ⟨⟨⟨_, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩

private theorem ok_link (P : GC.Hypotheses₂) {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type}
    [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ : G ℚ Ω}
    {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ} {Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)} (hG : Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb) (m C₀ : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O))
    (hU : UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L)) (hL : Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) (hg : barChar O Ψ g₁ ^ 2 ≠ 1)
    (hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L))
    (hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀) (hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m) :
    Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosure (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) (W.Lp L)⟩
  have hb := ok_basic l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL hg
  obtain ⟨h_del_open, h_csq, h_delc, h_loc, h_eps, h_Ψopen, h_Ψc, h_g0, h_card, h_cw, h_delcw,
    h_f2a, h_f2b, h_f2c, h_f3, h_f5, h_ΨN, h_dv, h_ne⟩ := hb
  have hf4 := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 1 (Or.inl rfl) (hS 1 (Or.inl rfl))
  have hf4' := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 2 (Or.inr rfl) (hS 2 (Or.inr rfl))
  rw [pow_one] at hf4
  obtain ⟨k, S, π, ι, pl, e, c, hF, hπ, hι⟩ := hL
  have hrk : GC.clRk l (((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) ⊓
      (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [rk_link l L η.ker (barChar O Ψ).ker]
    exact hC
  have hom : ∀ v : Φ.V, ∃ s, (Φ.om (ResidueField O)) ((Φ.loc v) s) ≠ 1 := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨g, hg'⟩ := k.mu v
    refine ⟨g, fun h => hg' ?_⟩
    rw [k.j_loc] at h
    simp only [Frame.om, MonoidHom.comp_apply, k.eps] at h
    have h1 := congrArg Units.val h
    simp only [Units.coe_map, Units.val_one, MonoidHom.coe_coe, RingHom.coe_comp,
      Function.comp_apply] at h1
    apply Units.ext
    simp only [cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe, Units.val_one]
    exact one_of_cast l (ResidueField O) _ h1
  have hfin : Finite (ResidueField O) := hG.fin
  have hok : Φ.Ok C₀ :=
    { l_odd := hG.odd
      fin := hfin
      del_open := h_del_open
      c_sq := h_csq
      del_c := h_delc
      loc_cont := h_loc
      eps_cont := h_eps
      Ψ_open := h_Ψopen
      Ψ_c := h_Ψc
      baseSubgroup_spec := h_g0
      phi := GC.phi _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      card_inf := h_card
      cw_sq := h_cw
      del_cw := h_delcw
      f2a := h_f2a
      f2b := h_f2b
      f2c := h_f2c
      f3 := h_f3
      f4 := hf4
      f4' := hf4'
      f5 := h_f5
      Ψ_N := h_ΨN
      gen := GC.gen P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hom
      cl := GC.cl _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      ec := GC.ec P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      b1p := GC.b1p _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      b1 := GC.b1 _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      h1 := GC.h1 P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hfin hom C₀ hrk }
  refine ⟨hok, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨v⟩ := h_ne
    have h1 : Φ.dv v ≤ Φ.d := by
      unfold Frame.d
      exact Finset.single_le_sum (fun _ _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ v)
    have := h_dv v
    omega
  · intro v
    have := h_dv v
    omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.frame_ex :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 → HasFrameW l η Ψ σ B M Q := by
  intro P Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨H₀, h0⟩ := exists_twistCharacter_continuousCohomology l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hwQ⟩ := auxw_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨C₀, hC₀⟩ := b0 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  set m := 3 * C₀ + 7 with hm_def
  have hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m := Nat.lt_pow_self hl.one_lt
  have hU := ufacts l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m
  obtain ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, hab, hup, hgal⟩ := field_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m _ hU
  have hM : B.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := by
    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    rw [ht, Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    have hle : B.valuation (t : Ω) ≤ 1 := B.valuation_le_one ⟨(t : Ω), natCast_mem B t⟩
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' hle) hG.over_l
  obtain ⟨s, ⟨hsD, hsU⟩, hs2⟩ := hU.f3 B hG.over_l
  have hsη : s ∈ η.ker := hU.split B hM ⟨hsD, hsU⟩
  have hs : s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω := by
    rw [hgal]
    exact ⟨hsU, hsη⟩
  rw [← hup] at hU
  obtain ⟨Φ, hL⟩ := link_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L s hU hab (by rw [hgal, hup]) hs
  have hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L) := by
    intro a ha
    rw [hup]
    exact strict P l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m a ha
  have hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [hup]
    exact hC₀ m
  obtain ⟨hok, hbig⟩ := ok_link P l hG m C₀ L s Φ hU hL hs2 hS hC hm
  refine ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, s, Φ, C₀, w, hL, hok, hbig, hw, hwQ, ?_⟩
  rw [hup]
  intro x hx
  exact hx.1.1.2


[MFC_SRC_GD_GEN] Full gen_gd remains a separate original input supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/SqrtDiscr.lean#L152-L284
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gen_gd :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Gen l D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs) ⊤ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ l _ c _ N _ e ψ D B hB
  have gd := D.gd
  obtain ⟨hcc2, hηcc, hΨcc, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, hIneL⟩ := dat_facts D
  obtain ⟨hηd, hd1, hψN, -⟩ := eta_killsMu D
  obtain ⟨⟨w, hw, huniq⟩, hwF⟩ := omT Ω l c
  obtain ⟨hwker1, hwcls, -, -, -, -, -, -⟩ := hwF w hw
  obtain ⟨w', η', a, hlit⟩ := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).1.mp gd
  have hrel := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).2 w' η' a hlit
  have hww : w' = w := huniq w' hlit.om
  subst hww
  have hbar : ∀ s, Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s) = omO Ω l c.O s := by
    intro s
    apply Units.ext
    exact RingHom.congr_fun (hom_ext ((residue c.O).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O))
      ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) (ResidueField c.O)).comp (PadicInt.toZModPow 1))) _
  have hPsi : ∀ s, D.Ψ s = chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s ^ e * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s := fun s => rfl
  have hbarPsi : ∀ s, barChar c.O D.Ψ s = omO Ω l c.O s ^ e * Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s) := by
    intro s
    show Units.map _ (D.Ψ s) = _
    rw [hPsi, map_mul, map_pow]
    congr 2
    exact hbar s
  have hbarw : ∀ s, Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (w' s) = omO Ω l c.O s := by
    intro s
    rw [← hbar s]
    apply Units.ext
    change residue c.O (w' s : c.O) = residue c.O (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s : c.O)
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mpr (hw.2 s)
  have homO : ∀ s, s ∈ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker → omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := by
    intro s hs
    show Units.map _ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s) = 1
    rw [hs, map_one]
  refine
    { odd := gd.l_odd
      fin := ?_
      M_ne := ?_
      l_dvd := ⟨N * (discr K).natAbs, by ring⟩
      two_dvd := ?_
      cc := D.conj
      over_l := hB
      quad := ?_
      cyc := ?_
      η_l := fun B' hB' => (hIneL B' hB').1
      even := ?_
      Ψ_l := ?_
      g4 := ?_
      g5 := ?_
      Hb_open := ?_
      Hb_ab := le_top
      Hb_idx := ⟨0, by simp⟩
      Hb_l := fun _ _ => le_top
      teich := ?_ }
  · haveI := c.dvr
    haveI := c.fin
    exact (abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).2.1
  · have hl := (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
    have hN := NeZero.ne N
    have hd : (discr K).natAbs ≠ 0 := by omega
    exact Nat.mul_ne_zero (Nat.mul_ne_zero hl hN) hd
  · exact Dvd.dvd.mul_right (Dvd.dvd.mul_left (even_iff_two_dvd.mp gd.N_even) l) _
  · obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
    obtain ⟨δ, hδ, hs⟩ := quad_ex gd.quad gd.imag D.η D.η_ker D.cc φ hφ hηcc
    exact ⟨_, δ, by omega, fun q _ hq => Dvd.dvd.mul_left hq _, hδ, hs⟩
  · refine ⟨1, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [pow_one]
      exact kills_mono (Dvd.intro_left _ rfl) hηd
    · rw [pow_one]
      intro s hs
      have hdiv : ∀ k, k ∣ l * N * (discr K).natAbs → ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ k = 1 → s ζ = ζ := by
        intro k hk ζ hζ
        apply hs
        obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hk
        rw [hm, pow_mul, hζ, one_pow]
      have h1 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 :=
        hψN s (hdiv N (Dvd.dvd.mul_right (Dvd.intro_left l rfl) _))
      haveI : NeZero l := ⟨(Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
      haveI : Fact (1 < l) := ⟨(Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt⟩
      have h2 : s ∈ (Chr.cls ℚ Ω l).ker := by
        rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
        apply (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω l).2.2.1
        intro ζ hζ
        rw [Units.val_one, ZMod.val_one, pow_one]
        exact hdiv l ⟨N * (discr K).natAbs, by ring⟩ ζ hζ
      rw [← hwcls, hwker1] at h2
      rw [hbarPsi, homO s h2, h1, one_pow, map_one, one_mul]
  · show Units.map _ (D.Ψ D.cc) = 1
    rw [hΨcc, map_one]
  · refine ⟨e + a, fun B' hB' s hs => ?_⟩
    rw [hbarPsi, hlit.ine B' hB' s hs, map_pow, hbarw s, pow_add]
  · intro hle
    apply gd.eta
    intro s hs
    obtain ⟨hs1, hs2⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hs
    rw [← D.η_ker]
    apply hle
    have hw1 : w' s = 1 := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, hwker1]
      exact hs1
    have hψ1 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 := by
      have h3 := hs2
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hrel] at h3
      simpa [hw1] using h3
    refine Subgroup.mem_inf.mpr ⟨homO s hs1, ?_⟩
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hbarPsi, homO s hs1, hψ1, one_pow, map_one, one_mul]
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, hu⟩ := (hIneL B hB).2
    refine ⟨s, hs.1, (hIneL B hB).1 hs, ?_⟩
    intro h
    have h' : residue c.O ((D.Ψ s : c.O) ^ 2) = residue c.O 1 := by
      have h4 := congrArg Units.val h
      simpa [barChar] using h4
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).mp (Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp h') hu
  · exact isOpen_univ
  · intro B' hB' s hs hb
    have hs' : s ∈ Ine B' := (Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hs).1
    have hz : cycZ ℚ Ω l s = 1 := cyc_ine Ω l B' hB' hs'
    have hc : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s = 1 := by
      show Units.map _ (cycZ ℚ Ω l s) = 1
      rw [hz, map_one]
    have hx : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 := by
      have hm : ¬ l ∣ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) := hlit.ord
      have hpow : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s ^ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) = 1 := by
        have h5 : (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ ^ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) s = 1 := by
          rw [pow_orderOf_eq_one, MonoidHom.one_apply]
        rwa [MonoidHom.pow_apply] at h5
      apply (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ hm hpow (one_pow _)
      rw [hbarPsi, ← hbar, hc, map_one, one_pow, one_mul] at hb
      have hb' : residue c.O (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s : c.O) = residue c.O 1 := by
        have h6 := congrArg Units.val hb
        simpa using h6
      exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp hb'
    rw [hPsi, hc, hx, one_pow, one_mul]


[MFC_SRC_HOST_GEN] Full gen_host remains separate from the CAF seed subset
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/GenHost.lean#L220-L255
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gen_host :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ → ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ Hb, Gen l (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N) Hb := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl c _ N _ e ψ o₁ hd σ hσ B hB
  have h2 : 2 ∣ l * N := hd.N_even.two_dvd.mul_left l
  refine ⟨(Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (kapB l ψ)).ker,
    { odd := hd.l_odd
      fin := ?_
      M_ne := mul_ne_zero hl.out.ne_zero (NeZero.ne N)
      l_dvd := dvd_mul_right _ _
      two_dvd := h2
      cc := hσ
      over_l := hB
      quad := ?_
      cyc := ⟨2, fun s hs => gh_dir_one _ (gh_cls_one 4 (pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd h2 2) hs), fun s hs => ?_⟩
      η_l := fun B' hB' s hs => gh_dir_one _ ((Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω 4).2.2.2.2 B'
        (gh_not_both B' (((Nat.coprime_primes hl.out Nat.prime_two).2 hd.l_odd).pow_right 2) hB') hs)
      even := ?_
      Ψ_l := gh_ine_l l c e ψ
      g4 := kernel_not_contained_eta l hd.l_odd c e ψ hd.four_le
      g5 := exists_decomposition_nonquadratic l c e ψ o₁ hd B hB
      Hb_open := Subgroup.isOpen_mono (fun s hs => gh_dir_one _ hs) (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1
      Hb_ab := Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _
      Hb_idx := gh_idx l ψ
      Hb_l := fun B' hB' => ((compIne Ω N l hl.out B' hB').2 c.O _).2.2 ?_
      teich := fun B' hB' s hs h => gh_teich l c e ψ B' hB' hs.1 hs.2 h }⟩
  · haveI := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1
    exact ResidueField.finite_of_finite (R := ℤ_[l]) (Finite.of_equiv _ PadicInt.residueField.symm.toEquiv)
  · obtain ⟨δ, h1, h3⟩ := gh_quad (Ω := Ω)
    exact ⟨1, δ, one_pos, fun q hq h => absurd (Nat.dvd_one.1 h) hq.one_lt.ne', h1, h3⟩
  · rw [gh_bar, gh_om_one l c.O (gh_cls_one _ (pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd_of_le (dvd_mul_right l N) one_le_two) hs), one_pow, one_mul,
      barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, gh_dir_one _ (gh_cls_one N ((dvd_mul_left N l).trans (dvd_pow_self _ two_ne_zero)) hs),
      map_one]
  · obtain ⟨w, hw, -⟩ := (omT Ω l c).1
    rw [barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ((psiBar Ω l c w hw N ψ).2.1 e).1 hd.par σ hσ, map_one]
  · obtain ⟨-, hmul, -, hinv, -, h6, -⟩ := compAlg c.O N l hl.out
    rw [kapB, kapL, hmul, hinv, (h6 _).1, mul_inv_cancel]
